what is next, defaults applied.

- Settings: eleven fields in `modules/employee_files/manifest.ts`, each `level: "owner"`: `askBank` and `askAddress` (on), `askEmergencyContact` and `askWorkLanguage` (off), `form101` (`print`, `link`, `off`), `form101Provider`, `form101Link` (https to a named host, its scheme and host kept in lower case), `payrollProvider`, `employerName`, `employerPhone` (a mobile or a landline, kept as E.164) and `deductionsFile` (nine digits from 9). `homeWidget` is `{ minRole: "worker" }`. `@domain/settings-form` gains the meta `entry` (`link`, `phone`, `digits`): `/manage` and the console draw such a text setting left to right with its keyboard, a phone as people write it, and the activity log says it left to right and "" as "לא נקבע".
- Migration `20261126000400_employee_files_settings.sql`: `private.employee_files_settings` (the manifest's defaults under what the business stored) and `private.file_asks`; `private.file_groups` replaced so `askAddress` and `askBank` decide the groups the file counts (a stored value that isn't a boolean reads as the default), so `file_missing`, the worker's read, the status and the export follow at once; `public.set_module_settings` replaced (20261120000200_order_taking_takers.sql's newest body, one branch after taking orders'): every field an owner's and the team's (42501), no key outside the eleven, each value one the manifest outputs (22023, `private.file_setting_line` and `private.file_setting_link`). Both keep their signatures; revoked and granted again all the same. `database.types.ts` doesn't change (no public signature does). The app works before it reaches a project: everything reads as asked, and a setting saves without the database's checks (the manifest's still apply; the module is off on the hosted project).
- The flow: `@domain/employee-file-settings` (the asks, `fileGroupsAsked`, `form101SetupOf`, the notice's names, `bankChangeToTell`, the seen cookie), `@domain/phone`'s `normalizeIsraeliPhone` and `displayIsraeliPhone`, and the steps' helpers taking the steps on screen (`fileParts`, `fileStepsOn`) with a state `optional`. The address and bank steps go when not asked, unless the file holds them (then said, and they can be changed or emptied); step 2 is "דרכי קשר" without the address, and takes the work language and the emergency contact (saved after the details by `saveEmergencyContact`, only when changed); step 5 by link shows "מילוי הטופס אצל {provider}" (`noopener noreferrer`, the site it opens under it) and "מסרתי את הטופס" marks it with the settings' route, decided on the server; with Form 101 off the step is gone and the done screen sends the file. The notice names the employer, whoever prepares the payroll, the 101's system and the phone. `/me`'s rows follow the parts, with a row for the contact.
- Today: the accent card "התיק שלי ב…" while something is missing (not for owners; flat when the first steps or an open mode hold the accent), else the quiet row says "הפרטים בתיק הושלמו"; a bank-change card for 14 days after `bank_changed_at`, with "לא שיניתי" (`disputeBankChangeAction`, then the card's second state and "הבנתי") and "אני שיניתי", kept per device in `ca_bank_seen` by the database's moment, read again on the server (`seenBankChangeAction`), forgotten on signing out. `currentFileToday` never takes Today down.

The notice says the managers see the emergency contact while step 2 shows it (`notice.emergency`), and the flow's and `/me`'s note say so too (`visibleEmergency`). A 101 system with no name set is named by its site in `<ltr>` (`provider` null; `form101.openSite`, `linkBodySite`, `notice.form101Site`), and the link's hint no longer has "https://" inside Hebrew (a tooling test holds every Hebrew address outside `<ltr>` to end on a letter or a digit). The done screen's send is said (`done.sent`) and its title takes the focus. The twin's `/manage` lists the modules at the level it draws (the owner's has the file), the activity twins as the owner, with a file's setting line (`textSettingShown` in `@domain/settings-form`, tested). The account no longer asked says so in its step and can be kept or changed; taking it out isn't on screen (ROADMAP 73 says so); `?ask=held` is that twin. The bank step on /me/file offers "לא שיניתי" under the saved account while the change is told, on every device (`FileScreenData.bankChange`, `BankChangeNote`), so hiding the card never takes the way to dispute. Who may write the file is shared by the actions and the route (`me/file/file-writer.ts`). A fund chosen "later" all that is left (`onlyPensionLeft`, `pensionLeftOf`): Today's card and the profile's say "נשאר לבחור קרן פנסיה עד …" with "בחירת קרן פנסיה" to `?step=pension`, and the done screen, once sent, is titled "נשאר לבחור קרן פנסיה" with that as its main action (before sending, the send stays first, since with Form 101 off the done screen is the only place the file goes, and it says the fund can come later: `done.readyPension`). The progress line counts an optional step only once it is done ("0 מתוך 4"), as the welcome and the bar count the steps. The file card's button is secondary when the card is flat, so the screen keeps one accent action. Form 101 by link: "מסרתי את הטופס" full width on a phone, as the link's button. Twins: Today `?file=pension` and `?file=complete`, the file `?state=later` (and `/me?file=later`), the file twin's way back by what the business asks, and Today's bank twin hides with its undo through a stand-in route that keeps nothing. Unit tests (the route, the actions' own moment, `stepsDone`, `onlyPensionLeft`, the cards, the done screen, the bank step, the progress, the 101 widths); e2e (the hold and its undo, a change made while the card was open told again, the cookie, "לא שיניתי" in the bank step, the second device) and design.spec.ts (four new URLs, three new walks, two updated).

"לא שיניתי" is never asked under the person's own change: the bank screen keeps `changedHere` apart from the account shown (`bankMoves`: confirmed sets it, "החלפת החשבון" and "השארת החשבון הנוכחי" leave it), and on a reload the seen cookie tells a change made on this device from one only said seen on Today (`ca_bank_seen` entries end `.here` for an account added here: `bankChangeSeenAs`, `FileScreenData.bankChange.seen`, read once in `currentFileScreen`, which Today now uses too), so the step still asks after "אני שיניתי", as the second review wanted, and not after the person's own account. ## 2026-11-22 · chat · sheets rise above the phone keyboard

- On chat/sheet-keyboard from origin/main 7d2f693. Orel's report of 22 November, on his phone: a small sheet with a field to type in opens the keyboard, and the keyboard covers the sheet. The cause: `Sheet` is `position: fixed` at the foot of the layout viewport, which iOS never shrinks for the keyboard and Android Chrome by default doesn't either (it shrinks only the visual viewport).
- `packages/ui/use-keyboard-lift.ts`: `keyboardLift` (pure) reads the two viewports, `layoutHeight - (visualViewport.height + offsetTop)`, never below 0, smaller as iOS moves the visual viewport down to a field, nothing under 40px, and nothing for a pinch (`scale` not 1); `watchKeyboard` follows the visual viewport's resize and scroll and the window's resize (turning the phone), says only changes, and once it has stood still 250ms with a keyboard up brings the field with the focus into sight within the sheet's body (`keepFocusInView`, `nearestScroll`: `block: "nearest"` by hand, since `scrollIntoView` would also move the page behind and the visual viewport); `useKeyboardLift` runs it while the sheet's scrim is drawn, the scrim's height being the layout viewport's (`innerHeight` follows the visual viewport on some browsers). No visualViewport, nothing lifted.
- `Sheet`: `--sheet-bottom` (8px over the keyboard) and `--sheet-max` (what is on screen, 8px from the top and its safe area) in its style, read by its classes with the old place as their fallback, so with no keyboard (a tablet, a desktop) nothing changes. `bottom` and `max-height` ease over 200ms, none with reduced motion; the drag and the way in and out keep the transform. Every sheet has it, ShareSheet, AttachSheet and the rest through `Sheet`. No `interactive-widget` in the viewport meta.
- Not changed: the chat's `Composer` is sticky in the conversation's own page, not fixed over a locked one, so the browser's own focus scroll takes it up as any field on a page, and no one has seen it covered; `FloatingDock` and the availability form's dock hold buttons, the tab bar links and the skeleton its placeholders, no field; `ChoiceMenu` is placed by Radix within the screen and has no field.
- Tests: unit (`use-keyboard-lift.test.ts`: iOS, Android, no keyboard, a browser that shrinks the layout viewport, iOS moving the visual viewport, a pinch, turning, rounding, the keyboard closing, the settle and the stop, no visualViewport, the field kept in sight; `sheet.test.ts`: the sheet drawn carries the lift, and nothing without it). design.spec.ts: "a sheet stands over the phone's keyboard (faked)", the phone only, `fakeKeyboard` shrinking `visualViewport.height` and sending its resize, since Playwright opens no keyboard.
- Checked here: `pnpm check`, and the dev server's /design/chat at 375px with the same faked keyboard (the sheet 8px over it and 8px under the top, its field in sight, moved by iOS's offset, nothing for a pinch, back down once closed, the field brought back into the body after the settle, the page behind not scrolled) and at the desktop's width (unchanged). Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright.
- Only a real phone can confirm: iOS Safari and the installed app, Android Chrome; that the lift follows the keyboard without a jump; iOS moving the visual viewport as well; and the chat's composer.
- Defaults applied: the gap over the keyboard 8px, as over the safe area; under a pinch nothing is lifted; the composer left as it is.
- After review: (1) a sheet opened from a button while a field on the page kept the keyboard (the chat's attach button on iOS) flew up to a keyboard about to close, then dropped about 300px during its opening spring. `watchKeyboard` now takes `within` (the sheet's Content, passed to `useKeyboardLift` as the scrim is) and lifts only while a field to type in inside it holds the focus (`opensKeyboard`: an input that is not a button, box, file and the like, a textarea or contenteditable, not read-only), read again on `focusin` and on `focusout` by its `relatedTarget`, so going from one field to the next is no drop; a field that autofocuses (CodeSlots) is inside at once and lifts. (2) Sideways, with some 130px over the keyboard, the header and the `shrink-0` footer left the body no height: the field vanished and the footer went under the keyboard. Below `SHEET_FRAME_MIN` (220px: header, a footer with a large button, one field) `sheetScrollsWhole` makes the Content scroll whole (`overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain`, the body `shrink-0`), and the settle brings the field into sight in the body, then in the sheet (`keepFocusInSheet`). (3) `nearestScroll`'s comment and test now say what it does: a field taller than the view aligns its top when below and its bottom when above (CSSOM). Unit tests for each; checked on the dev server with the faked keyboard: the attach sheet opened from the focused composer is never lifted, the close button or a radio taking the focus drops it, and at 844x360 with 130px left the new message sheet and the choices sheet (its quantity in the footer) scroll whole, the field 12px over the sheet's foot.

## 2026-11-22 · chat · the worker's hiring file on the phone (profile H5a, #82)

- On chat/hiring-file-me from origin/main 79bbfee (H4's branch chat/hiring-file-db, PR #218, with main merged in; H4 not merged yet). The design's §0, §1.2, §1.3, §2.4, §2.5, §3.1, §3.2, §3.5, §4.3, §5's H5 row and §6, with Orel's decisions of 8 November on #1.
- The H5 row was two reviews' worth, so it is split: H5a here, the worker's flow with the SMS step-up and no migration; H5b next, the module's settings with their migration, Today's cards, landing on the welcome after `/join` and former workers' files. The step-up is H5a's, not H5b's as suggested: H4's `set_employee_details` refuses any bank write without a fresh `private.step_ups` row, so a bank step without the code can't save at all.
- `/me/file`: the welcome (a new file), step 0 (the file's language and the form of address, both on the profile), identity (an Israeli ID, a passport or another document, the names as on it, the birth date, and the medical-certificate card as soon as the date says under 18 today), address (the town from data.gov.il's 1,316 settlements, searched in either language and spelled either way; a street, or a place or P.O. box with "אין שם רחוב"), the account (pasted and split, Masav's check in words with "המספר נכון, לשמור", whose account, the written instruction in the chosen form, a code by SMS to the person's own number), the pension (the nine comprehensive funds of the Authority's October 2026 data, "אין לי את השם עכשיו", the business's choice, "אבחר אחר כך" until the due day), Form 101 on paper with "מסרתי את הטופס", "שליחת הפרטים", and done. A reason under every field, "לא חובה" beside the optional ones, the notice "למה מבקשים את הפרטים האלה" one tap away on every step, the saved ID and account as their last digits with "הצגה" for a minute. Phone: one step under the steps bar, its main action floating above the tab bar; tablet: the steps beside the one on screen; desktop: every step and a sticky summary with the page's one main action.
- English for the file alone: `englishWording`, a nested provider with the file's words only (`fileMessages`, held by `file-messages.test.ts` to what its client parts read) and a `dir="ltr" lang="en"` frame that keeps the business's colour; the rest of the app stays Hebrew. `profiles.locale` is read into `OwnProfile` and taken by `checkProfileChange`.
- The way in: `/me` shows the accent card "חסרים עוד N פרטים" with "השלמת הפרטים" (not for owners) and "התיק שלי ב{business}", five rows to the steps. The gate: a manifest `page` (`/me/file`, worker and up) that `canOpenModule` and `moduleWidgets` read for a module with no tab, so order taking and notifications keep their rules.
- Server: `app/(app)/me/file/actions.ts`, each bound to the screen's business, the person always the session's, never while viewing: a step's save, the person's own reveal, the bank's code (`sendCode` and `verifyCode` from `app/(auth)/otp.ts`, counted with the sign-in codes, to `own_phone` only, `noteStepUp` only once the code was right and the person's, then the save), "מסרתי את הטופס" (route `print`) and the send. `@db/employee-files` passes up the day a bank change opens again after a phone change (`opensOn`, the refusal's DETAIL when it is a date).

UI: `Steps`, `IdField`, `BankField`, `SearchField` with `CityField` and `CountryField`, `MaskedValue`, `StepRow`'s `current`, `ChoiceMenu`'s list scrolling within the screen; the gallery's "תיק העובד" and the twin `/design/shell/me/file` (`?step`, `?state`, `?lang=en`) and `/design/shell/me?file=`.
- The local demo restaurant takes file writes (`"fileWrites": true`); the shop and the hosted ones stay closed (`tooling/demo-files.test.ts`). The module stays off everywhere: the e2e switches it on and off itself and gives Noa her made-up file back.

the file is sent only once identity, address, bank and pension are in (`sendBlocker`, Form 101 on paper never holds it): until then the send action is "השלמת …" and leads to the first open one, a desktop's welcome has its own "התחלת המילוי" in the accent with the summary's action quiet beside it, and the done screen says "סיימת" only with nothing open (else "נשארו עוד N צעדים") with its main action, the first open step or "חזרה לפרופיל", in the dock and on a desktop; `FloatingDock` names the bar's side logically (`start-`/`end-` under `rtl:`/`ltr:`), and `tooling/logical-utilities.test.ts` now finds any physical utility (one exception, `ltrInputClass`); the bank's code goes to `auth.users.phone` (`signInPhoneOf`), is checked on a client that keeps no session (`createDetachedClient`, `checkCode`), whose session is ended at once, and whose account is compared before anything is kept; the account is tried before any SMS (`checkBankChange`: the write dated 15 minutes on, past any code, so it stops at the code and keeps nothing), so the week after a phone change says its day, sends nothing and holds the action (the twin's `?state=hold`); English town names typed without the list's apostrophe are found ("Raanana", "Beer Sheva"); a number shown hides when its screen is left, kept in an `<Activity>` or not (`watchShown`); a saved document's new country, expiry or name asks for its number again; passport and document numbers, names, the document's name, the email and a relative's name say their lengths under their fields; "השארת החשבון הנוכחי" goes back to the saved account; a move with this year's 101 in says the updated one is due (`changeDue`, shared), and the twin's `done` shows it. "קצר מדי" says what the number needs. One migration after all (`20261126000300_bank_change_hold.sql`): `public.bank_change_hold` reads the week after a phone change at the real time, since the try dated 15 minutes on missed a week ending in between and spent the code; `checkBankChange` asks it after the try and passes the moment (`opensAt`), and the step holds through the day it opens, lets go at that moment and says a week ending later today by its time ("היום מ-…"). ## 2026-11-21 · chat · taking orders: offline (waiter mode P7)

- On chat/order-offline from origin/main e5a0d06 (P6's branch, PR #213, on top of main e988dd8 with P1 to P5 merged). Orel's decisions of 20 November: every phase, §16's recommendations. No migration: `database.types.ts` unchanged, no pgTAP file, no Migrate run.
- The outbox (`app/(app)/orders/outbox.ts`): one per page, outside React, the only writer of the drafts, the rounds on their way, their refusals and the menu kept on the phone. The mode's provider reads it through `useSyncExternalStore`, so a mode hidden in an `<Activity>` or drawn afresh reads the same, and P4's `claim`/`keep` is gone. `OrdersOutbox` in the `(app)` layout starts it for whoever takes orders, so a killed app sends what waited as it opens, on any screen. A round goes at once and again at 1, 2, 5, 10 and 30 s, woken by `online` and the page shown, with one last keepalive as it hides (64 KB in all until shown again, once each); it says "עוד לא נשלח" only once its strict write is done, and the screen's send lets go then, so another may follow offline. After 10 minutes it checks one's own rounds (`order_mine`, whose ids are the keys) and asks: "שליחה" (again after another 10) or "הסרה מהטלפון" with "ביטול". A refusal goes back to its draft's front with its reason in one transaction with the settle, once across tabs. 401 and `notMember` take the person's rounds and menu off the phone (all businesses, or that one), drafts kept, with a one-time notice of place names in sessionStorage (`orders-lost`) on the app's banner, the door and the no-business screen; 409 `person` holds them in the page and draws the frame again.
- The store (`orders-db.ts`): IndexedDB `conceptapp-orders` v1, `rounds`, `drafts` (with the refusal and whether its screen showed it) and `menus`, each record with its person and business, read back through `@domain/orders-outbox`'s schemas; other tabs hear through `BroadcastChannel`; `memoryOrdersDb` where IndexedDB is missing or blocked, and in the tests. Another person's rounds, and any draft or menu, go after 16 hours untouched; one's own never silently.
- The server: `sendBody` gains a required `user`; `/orders-send` refuses another than the session's (409 `person`) before `viewOnly`, and a business not among `getOwnPlace`'s new `memberOf` (403 `notMember`), both before the database.
- Session ends (`orders-storage.ts`, which imports only the store): signing out asks once when an order of the person's hasn't gone ("יציאה ומחיקה" / "חזרה"), then clears their records in every business and what expired, awaited before the page goes; locking the clock station clears everyone's, after a warning line with how many and where. Both remove P4's `orders:` sessionStorage entries, which the outbox's first open also drops.
- Screens: a round on its way is "בשליחה", "עוד לא נשלח" or the question (`RoundState`, also over the items on a phone); the phone opens on the order when a round is on its way; the floor's tile "לא נשלח" (`PlaceTile` tone `unsent`), first on the status line, in the desktop table and the order's list of places, and the new takeaway's tile while its first round waits; a count on "שולחנות"; "אין חיבור" (`ConnectionNote`, §7.4's words) under the title of the floor, the order, "ההזמנות שלי", the prep screen and the till; outside the mode a banner above the screen (`OutboxBannerView`, never a toast) with "פתיחה", and each round that lands said to screen readers. The menu is kept on the phone and seeds the mode at once.
- The sync's pace (`ordersSyncPace`): 5 s while a round of one's own of an open order has a ticket at its department with lines left (doubling to 60), and on the prep screen (doubling to 30); else 15.
- Pure: `@domain/orders-outbox` (`rowTurn`, `askAge`, `sendVerdict`, `keepsRecord`, `sentAlready`, the schemas), `ordersSyncPace`, `nextOrdersSyncDelay`'s `pace`. The twin: states `asking`, `offline` and `lost`, its sender's `answer`/`remove`/`unsent`, five index rows; the gallery's "לא נשלח" tile.
- Tests: unit for the domain, the sync's pace and body, the route (`user` required, `person`, `notMember`), `getOwnPlace`'s `memberOf`, the store in memory, the engine (`outbox.test.ts`: kept before waiting, offline then online once, the retry waits, a refusal back and kept, another tab's settle, the question, `answer`, `remove`/`restore`, a round that went already, another person, a session switch, `person`, viewing, lost by 401 and by `notMember`, the hide's keepalive and its budget, expiry, another tab's message, a change before the first read, the menu, no store), the session ends, the send store's new fields, the twin, and a toast case; e2e `orders-offline.spec.ts` (five flows) with the shared helpers moved to `e2e/orders-parts.ts`, `orders.spec.ts`'s direct sends with `user` and the new refusals, `e2e/db.ts` `demoRoundSenders`, design.spec five URLs and a walk. Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run here: Playwright: first in CI.
- Changed from the plan: the refusal kept on a draft also keeps where its round went, so the banner names its place; keepalive's 64 KB are shared until the page shows again, not per hide; a round a screen waits for settles by its own answer rather than by the sync's `mine` (one toast); the sign-out sheet's title is `outbox.unsent` with the places under it, and its close button is "חזרה"; the station lock reads what hasn't gone as its sheet opens.
- For Orel, not applied (CLAUDE.md): `PlaceTile`'s `unsent` tone, `OutboxBannerView`/`LostOrdersNoticeView` and `ConnectionNote` for "Built so far".
- Defaults applied (to ask Orel): drafts kept on the phone per person and business until sent or 16 hours; sign-out asks once and says what goes; another person's waiting order waits for them, up to 16 hours, shown to nobody else; "הסרה מהטלפון" removes with "ביטול" for 8 seconds; after "שליחה" another 10 minutes asks again; every 5 seconds while a round of one's own is in preparation and on the prep screen; no other table offline in P7; a banner outside the mode, no toast; a 401 removes the person's waiting orders with a notice; another business's order waits for that business; locking the station removes everything after saying how many; "אין חיבור" as a card under the title. The lost notice shows where the refresh keeps the app's frame too: `keepLostNotice` says so on the window and `LostOrdersNotice` reads through `useSyncExternalStore` (`lost-notice.ts`); it has its own name (`outbox.lost.label`, two asides were one landmark), and is mounted after the outbox, whose live region comes first and is `aria-live` without `role="status"` (schedule-build.spec's status locator), so a card stays right above `main` and its glow. The sign-out sheet keeps its words while it closes. - P7's demo (§14) in the product: "switching demo person keeps it unsent" holds where a session ends without signing out here (expired, or the cookies cleared, as the e2e does), since the door opens only signed out and signing out asks and takes the person's orders off the phone. - After the re-review of those fixes (each finding confirmed):
  - A new takeaway's first round: its send waits while that round is on its way, whichever send it was (`sendWaits`), so a screen shown again, mounted afresh or reloaded opens no second takeaway; a round that went again with no screen waiting says "נשלחה" with "פתיחה" once it lands (never over a way back), and the screen that showed it on its way goes to its order (`sender.landed`); a send answered after its screen was left gets "פתיחה" too.
  - The tab's entry has one writer, the mode that read it last (`claim`/`keep` in orders-storage.ts): one Next dropped from its cache settles a late answer in its own memory only.
  - A ticket put back here stays out of the ready news until a sync started after the put-back answered has come (`readyMarks`, ready-marks.ts), so a slower sync from before it says nothing.
  - The mode's toast is a pure controller now (`modeToast`, mode-toast.ts): a withdrawal takes away its own toast alone and tells the gate at once, an older toast seen going after a newer one was said lets no news out, and the tests hold it (a newer serve's "ביטול" kept, the withdrawn take-back's write unheard); the news-gate test that only repeated the gate's path is gone.
- After the re-review of a898aab (each finding confirmed):
  - A send answered after its screen was left says "נשלחה" through the news gate (`tell` on the mode's value), so it never takes the place of a take-back's or a serve's "ביטול" on the screen now shown.
  - A withdrawn toast lets the news out only once what withdrew it is done (a microtask): the next take-back's toast, said in the same turn, keeps the ready news waiting instead of hiding it.
  - The gate keeps what waits by what it is about ("ready", `sent:<round>`) and tells each in turn, the next once the toast before it has gone, so a round that landed and the ready news no longer drop each other.
  - A ticket marked ready here is news again once a read made after the mark sees it not ready (`readyMarks.seen`): put back and made ready again on another phone, its owner is told.
- After the re-review of 8731add (each finding confirmed): a serve's "ביטול" is no ready news (`readyNews` leaves out a ticket served before and ready again with the time it was ready at; one served new and marked ready since still is); a toast whose time is up is forgotten (`modeToast.hidden` puts null), and a `StatusToast` drawn again with the same message (its screen shown again from a hidden `<Activity>`) goes on with the time it had left and never takes the focus again, so no finished "מוכן" or stale "ביטול" comes back on returning to the mode; the send's "נשלחה" on its own screen goes through the news gate too, so a take-back's or a serve's "ביטול" said while the round was on its way stays until it goes.

- The demo walkthrough of §14 P3, not run here (no Docker): for the next session, after `pnpm db:start`, in psql as postgres against the local seed. It rolls back. Each comment says what the plan expects, not what was seen.

  ```sql
  begin;

  create function pg_temp.b() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-b000-000000000001'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.ori() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000001'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.shiran() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000002'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.noa() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000003'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.yoav() returns uuid language sql as $$ select '00000000-0000-4000-a000-000000000004'::uuid $$;
  create function pg_temp.item(item_name text) returns uuid language sql as $$
    select i.id from public.menu_items i
    where i.business_id = pg_temp.b() and i.name = item_name and i.archived_at is null
  $$;
  create function pg_temp.place(place_name text) returns uuid language sql as $$
    select p.id from public.order_places p
    where p.business_id = pg_temp.b() and p.name = place_name and p.archived_at is null
  $$;
  create function pg_temp.refusal(call text) returns text language plpgsql as $$
  declare got_code text; got_hint text;
  begin
    execute call;
    return 'none';
  exception when others then
    get stacked diagnostics got_code = returned_sqlstate, got_hint = pg_exception_hint;
    return concat_ws(' ', got_code, nullif(got_hint, ''));
  end;
  $$;

  -- Taking orders on, as the team would switch it in the console; Shiran and Yoav on the clock.
  update public.business_modules set enabled = true
  where business_id = pg_temp.b() and module_key = 'order_taking';
  select private.order_taking_settings(pg_temp.b()) -> 'takerDepartments' as takers;  -- ["floor", "bar"]
  insert into public.time_entries (business_id, user_id, clock_in)
  values (pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.yoav(), now() - interval '2 hours'),
         (pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.shiran(), now() - interval '3 hours');

  -- 1. A send split into the kitchen and the bar.
  select public.send_order_round(pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
    jsonb_build_object('place', pg_temp.place('2')),
    jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('חומוס'), 'quantity', 2, 'note', 'בלי שום', 'allergy', true),
      jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('בירה מהחבית'), 'quantity', 1)));
  select d.key as department, t.status, l.name, l.quantity, l.unit_agorot, l.allergy
  from public.order_lines l
  join public.order_tickets t on t.id = l.ticket_id
  join public.departments d on d.id = t.department_id
  where t.round_id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'
  order by d.key;  -- bar: בירה מהחבית 1 × 2800; kitchen: חומוס 2 × 3200, allergy

  -- 2. The same key twice: the same answer, one round; another body: refused.
  select public.send_order_round(pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
    jsonb_build_object('place', pg_temp.place('2')),
    jsonb_build_array(
      jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('חומוס'), 'quantity', 2, 'note', 'בלי שום', 'allergy', true),
      jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('בירה מהחבית'), 'quantity', 1)));
  select count(*) as rounds from public.order_rounds where id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001';  -- 1
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001',
       jsonb_build_object('place', %L::uuid), jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 3)))$q$,
    pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.place('2'), pg_temp.item('חומוס')));  -- 23505 body

  -- 3. Yoav takes back the beer he sent to his own bar: refused, he cancels with a reason instead.
  select pg_temp.refusal(format('select public.take_back_order_line(%L, %L)', pg_temp.yoav(),
    (select l.id from public.order_lines l join public.order_tickets t on t.id = l.ticket_id
     where t.round_id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and l.name = 'בירה מהחבית')));  -- 42501 own_department

  -- 4. A taker's cancel, in the log with its price.
  select public.cancel_order_line(pg_temp.shiran(),
    (select l.id from public.order_lines l join public.order_tickets t on t.id = l.ticket_id
     where t.round_id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' and l.name = 'בירה מהחבית'), 'guest');
  select a.details from public.audit_log a
  where a.business_id = pg_temp.b() and a.action = 'order.line.cancel'
  order by a.created_at desc limit 1;  -- name, quantity 1, unitAgorot 2800, where {"place": "2"}, reason guest, sender Yoav

  -- 5. A close settles the kitchen's new ticket as served; a late round is then refused as closed.
  select public.close_order(pg_temp.yoav(),
    (select r.order_id from public.order_rounds r where r.id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'));
  select t.status, t.served_new from public.order_tickets t
  where t.round_id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001' order by t.status;  -- served, true (both)
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002',
       jsonb_build_object('order', %L::uuid, 'place', %L::uuid),
       jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 1)))$q$,
    pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(),
    (select r.order_id from public.order_rounds r where r.id = 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'),
    pg_temp.place('2'), pg_temp.item('מים')));  -- 55000 closed

  -- 6. Two first sends to table 3 twenty seconds apart join one order; a third two minutes on is told it's taken.
  select public.send_order_round(pg_temp.shiran(), pg_temp.b(), 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003',
    jsonb_build_object('place', pg_temp.place('3')),
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('לימונדה'), 'quantity', 1)), now());
  select public.send_order_round(pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004',
    jsonb_build_object('place', pg_temp.place('3')),
    jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', pg_temp.item('פוקאצ''ה'), 'quantity', 1)),
    now() + interval '20 seconds');
  select count(distinct r.order_id) as orders from public.order_rounds r
  where r.id in ('a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003', 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000004');  -- 1
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000005',
       jsonb_build_object('place', %L::uuid), jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 1)),
       now() + interval '2 minutes')$q$,
    pg_temp.ori(), pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.place('3'), pg_temp.item('מים')));  -- 23505 taken

  -- 7. Yoav clocked out 31 minutes ago: refused; 29 minutes after his clock-out he still could.
  update public.time_entries set clock_out = now() - interval '31 minutes'
  where business_id = pg_temp.b() and user_id = pg_temp.yoav() and clock_out is null;
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000006',
       jsonb_build_object('place', %L::uuid), jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 1)))$q$,
    pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.place('9'), pg_temp.item('מים')));  -- 42501 clock
  select pg_temp.refusal(format(
    $q$select public.send_order_round(%L, %L, 'a3000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000007',
       jsonb_build_object('place', %L::uuid), jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('item', %L::uuid, 'quantity', 1)),
       now() - interval '2 minutes')$q$,
    pg_temp.yoav(), pg_temp.b(), pg_temp.place('9'), pg_temp.item('מים')));  -- none

  -- 8. Noa, signed in: no direct read of the lines; her prep view has her kitchen's tickets and no price.
  select set_config('request.jwt.claims', json_build_object('sub', pg_temp.noa(), 'role', 'authenticated')::text, true);
  set local role authenticated;
  select pg_temp.refusal('select count(*) from public.order_lines');  -- 42501
  select public.order_prep(pg_temp.b()) -> 'tickets';  -- table 4's kitchen ticket and table 3's focaccia; no "unitAgorot"
  reset role;

  rollback;
  ```

  The items מים, לימונדה and פוקאצ'ה and tables 2, 3 and 9 come from P1's sample; the sample orders hold tables 4 and 7, which it leaves alone.
- Next: database-reviewer, security-reviewer and business-reviewer on the PR; then P4, the send and the kitchen's screens.

- With P2 (#208) merged in: `20261120000500_order_taking_roles_hint.sql` writes the restaurant's and café's own sentence for the roles screen's hint again, with P3's base (taking orders, cancelling, "ready", on the house), only where the team hasn't changed P2's; the menu twin's copy of the restaurant's words follows (`tooling/template-wording.test.ts`). P3's `reset_demo_business` is P1's newest body plus the one seed line, checked by a diff.

- Orel's decision of 20.11, recorded on #1: on a device that is the business's clock station (#78 phase 5, #192), signing out sends it back to `/station` with the code, not to the door, and anyone signed in on it sees on Home that it is the business's tablet, to sign out when done. Whoever edits the clock keeps "נעילת המכשיר". On chat/station-signout, from main `879f5db`. - Signing out: its body moved unchanged to `app/(auth)/end-session.ts` (`endSessionHere`, server-only and not "use server", so it is no endpoint). `signOut` ends the session first and only then asks `signedOutPath`, whose failed read is the door; the path is always one of two constants. `lockDeviceAction` calls `endSessionHere` and still answers `/station` itself. (Orel's words; `app.clock.station.reminder.signOutBody`) with a secondary "יציאה מהחשבון".

## 2026-11-20 · chat · the clock's shift today, clear of midnight

- On chat/clock-last-minute, from main `05cd369`. `clock.spec.ts` "the clock knows the person's own shift today, and nobody else's" failed in CI on 17 November at 23:59:10 Israel time: its all-day shift (00:00–23:59) had just ended, so /clock said "אין לך עוד משמרת היום". Over midnight, today can also turn between putting the shift on and reading the screen. `shift-swaps.spec.ts` "a shift that has begun today offers no giving away" has the same all-day shift and that second risk (its screen lists shifts from today on); no other spec has one.
- `waitOutDemoMidnight(testInfo)` in `e2e/db.ts`: when midnight at the demo restaurant, on its own clock in the database, is closer than the test's own time and two minutes, it waits until midnight has passed and adds the wait to the test's time. Both tests call it before `scheduleDemoToday` and keep their all-day shift and their checks (the person's own hours, the manager with none and not Noa's; the begun shift with no giving away). Never a skip: near midnight only, a wait of at most about two and a half minutes.
- Not a shift around now: the swaps screen would still lose it over midnight, so it needed the wait anyway, and the all-day shift reads the same and takes the same path on every run.
- After review: every other caller of `scheduleDemoToday` reads its shift on a screen too, so over midnight it is yesterday's. `profile.spec.ts` "a person sets a photo from Today, and colleagues see it" (the manager reads who works today on /home tens of seconds on), `today.spec.ts` "everyone reads who works today" and `schedule-build.spec.ts` "a manager shares the published week, and its link opens that week" (Noa's shift today on the schedule tab, next week as next week) now wait out midnight at their start too. `scheduleDemoToday` throws in a test that has not called `waitOutDemoMidnight` (a `WeakSet` of its `TestInfo`, read through `test.info()`), so a new caller that forgets fails on every run, not only on the night it crosses midnight.
- Checked here: `pnpm check`. Not run: Playwright (no Docker here); CI runs the five tests at 390, 820 and 1440.
- Next: nothing from this. Defaults applied: none.
## 2026-11-20 · chat · CI: the local stack's port taken on a fresh runner

- On chat/ci-supabase-port from main (`05cd369`), under the night protocol (no questions). Nine jobs from 9 to 18 November failed at the stack's very first start, seconds after its images were pulled, on "failed to bind host port 0.0.0.0:54322/tcp: address already in use" (the database job three times, e2e six, five of them the phone job, on different runners and regions). #152's retry 15 seconds later failed the same way all four times it ran; every rerun on another runner passed. Not the stack's own containers: there were none before, and the CLI removes them on a failed start ("Stopping containers..." before its error). Nothing outside holds 54322 every time, so `supabase/config.toml` keeps its ports. Likeliest cause: 54320 to 54329 lie inside Linux's ephemeral range, so an outgoing connection (the pull's, most likely) can have 54322 as its own, open or for 60 seconds in TIME_WAIT.
- `.github/scripts/supabase-start.sh`, called by both jobs. `reserve`, a new step right after checkout, puts the config's ports in `net.ipv4.ip_local_reserved_ports`, so no later connection takes one. The start (`db start`, or `start -x …`) on a taken port prints `sudo ss -ltnp` and every socket on the port, `docker ps -a` and the networks, removes this project's containers and network, ends a docker-proxy left on the port, drops its TIME_WAIT sockets, waits up to 90 s until it is free, and tries again after 5, 10 and 20 s, four attempts in all, then `::error::`. Any other failure still fails at once.
- Checked: the script against stubs of pnpm, docker, ss, sysctl and sudo (taken twice then free, busy past 90 s, kong's port on the second attempt, not a port, taken every time), its parsing against the real log of run 44188825889, and `pnpm check`. Not run here: CI itself (no Docker).
- Next: CI shows the reserve step and a clean start; if a port is taken again, the printout says by whom.
- Defaults applied: the ports kept from outgoing connections in a step of their own, beyond the retry asked for.

## 2026-11-17 · chat · team: fixing a name on /team/[person] (per business)

- On chat/team-name-fix, from main `b206f49`. The ROADMAP row "תיקון שם": whoever may change someone's role fixes the name the business shows for them. The name is the business's own for them (`public.member_names`), shown everywhere in that business, to the person too, and nowhere else; `profiles.full_name` is never touched.
- Why per business: one login is in several businesses (an accountant in two; the seed's Galit in both demos), so rewriting `profiles.full_name` from one business would change what another shows, and refusing it only for people in another business would tell the manager they are (#80). The clock starts at 20:42 on a Thursday and runs with real time, for the ages and the 2-minute take-back.

- On chat/clock-breaks-screen, stacked on chat/clock-cancel-screen (PR4, #189, not merged yet); the database (PR2, `20261117000300_break_edits.sql`) is on main and migrated. - `@db/time-clock`: `addTimeBreak`, `changeTimeBreak`, `removeTimeBreak` and `restoreTimeBreak` with the secret key (refusals pass their hint on, as the cancel's), and `getTimeEntry(business, entry)`, one shift with its live breaks under the person's session, to read typed times against.
- `@domain/time-clock`: `ShiftRecord.breaks` (`BreakRecord` {id, start, end | null, minutes, unpaid}, earliest first, live only, none for a cancelled shift); `parseBreakTimes(entry, {start, end}, timeZone, now)`: HH:MM, the start the first moment at or after the clock-in with that time (from the clock-in's own minute, the clock-in itself), the end the first after the start, so a break past midnight takes no date; `breakTimes`, `breakSameTime`, `future` and `breakOutside` (one before the clock-in can only be the next day). `clockFailureOf`: `breakOutside` (22023 hint), `breakTimes` (the other 22023s of `check_break_fits`), `breakOverlap` (23P01 on `time_breaks_no_overlap`), `breakOpen` and `tooLate` (55000 hints), `breakGone` (55000 "The break is removed", P0002 "Unknown break"), `notRemoved`; `punchFailureOf` reads the six as `failed`.
- App: `EntryActions` gains `addBreak`, `changeBreak`, `removeBreak`, `restoreBreak`; `EntryTarget` `break` (`pause`, null to add); `EntryFailure` the break kinds, `ownBreaks` and `tooLate`; `PersonHoursData.unpaidBreaks`. `actions.ts`: the four behind `clockEditor` (and `z.uuid()`), the times parsed against the shift read by `getTimeEntry`, then `parseReason`, `pushSoon` and `refresh`; `ownHours` reads `ownBreaks`, a restore already back is fine. `person-view.tsx`: under the shift's times, "הפסקות" (a group named by its heading), a row each ("13:00–13:30 · 30 דק׳", "לא בתשלום"), one still running not pressable, and "הוספת הפסקה". The break's step: the shift (`ShiftSummary`), start and end (`type="time"`, `dir="ltr"`, `ltrInputClass`), whether it comes off the hours, "למה מתקנים", "שמירת ההפסקה" / "הוספת ההפסקה" (the sheet closes with "ההפסקה נשמרה" / "ההפסקה נוספה"), a danger-ghost "הסרת ההפסקה" with the same reason (closes, "ההפסקה הוסרה" with "ביטול" for 10 seconds, its own transition, the cancel's focus pattern; if it can't, the shift opens saying why, or the toast says it over a sheet opened meanwhile), and "חזרה לתיקון השעות" (the fix as typed, the focus on the row that led there).
- Twins: `demoAddBreak`, `demoChangeBreak` (`parseBreakTimes` against the demo shift, and its other breaks for an overlap), `demoRemoveBreak`, `demoRestoreBreak`; Yoav's evening has two breaks and his shift today one still running; `?state=break|addBreak|openBreak`. Every ShiftRecord literal has `breaks` (the clock demo's, Yoav's and Noa's, the pay demo's cancelled one).
- Messages: `app.clock.team.breaks.*` (title, row, rowOpen, unpaid, running, add), `breakStep.*` (editTitle, addTitle, editSubtitle, addSubtitle, start, end, hint, unpaid, paid, newUnpaid, newPaid, save, saveAdd, remove, back), `done.changeBreak`, `addBreak`, `removeBreak`, `restoreBreak`, `failure.breakTimes`, `breakSameTime`, `breakOutside`, `breakOverlap`, `breakOpen`, `breakGone`, `ownBreaks`, `tooLate`.
- Tests: unit `parseBreakTimes` (the day, past midnight, the clock-in's minute, outside, open shifts, bad input), `monthRecords`' breaks, `clockFailureOf` for every refusal of the four, `punchFailureOf`, `getTimeEntry` (2525 pass). e2e (first run in CI): `clock-team.spec.ts` (a break's end changed, without why refused with no log row, the total up, the shift "תוקן על ידי"; a forgotten break added with `unpaidBreaks` on, the total down, refused before the clock-in and over another break with no log row; both log lines with their reasons; the worker reads the fix; a removal and the toast's undo, both log rows; a break still running opens nothing), with `startDemoBreak` in e2e/db.ts and `unpaidBreaks` set back in `startClean`; design.spec (the three states clean in both appearances, and the twin's refusals, back, save, removal and undo).
- Docs: ROADMAP's clock row (✅, the break edit described) and the pilot's list (nothing left "חובה לפיילוט"), the db README. AGENT.md:243 still says the break edit is PR5: left for the main session.
- Review (3 lenses, each finding verified): four, fixed. A break from the clock-in's own minute was sent as the clock-in cut to milliseconds, which the database refuses as before it (a punched clock-in keeps microseconds): it now sends the clock-in's own string. In the hour the clocks repeat, typed times took the second of the two, moving an unchanged break an hour: `parseBreakTimes` now takes every real reading and the earliest from the clock-in, with tests. Saving or removing a break closed the sheet and dropped shift times typed but not saved: the break rows wait, with "קודם שומרים אותן", while the times differ. `failure.breakTimes` no longer says the end must be after the start, which the hint above it contradicts. Checked on a build at 390 and 1440, axe clean. Refuted: a database "future" shown as breakTimes (the screen says future first); the undo's refusal words.
- Second review (2 lenses, verified): four more, fixed. A changed break re-read its times by the minute, so an unchanged start moved: refused as an overlap when another break ended in the same minute, and in the hour the clocks repeat a break in the second of the two moved to the first (and could count an hour too long). `parseBreakTimes` now takes the break being changed (`keep`, from `changeBreakAction`) and keeps a time left as it shows as stored, and a start typed in the minute another break ended starts where that one ended; tests for both. The "save first" gate measured from the live record, so a refresh locked the rows: it measures from the times the sheet opened on. Its note is a polite status that the rows' group points to (WCAG 4.1.3). Checked on a build at 390, 820 and 1440, axe clean.
- Third review (2 lenses, verified): five, small, fixed. A start in a minute where two breaks ended now follows the later end (the first in the database's order could land the new break over the second); the clock-in's minute follows a break that ended in it too; a changed break's new start is the reading nearest the start it had, so in the repeated hour it stays on that side of the change (a final check found the first try, the last reading before a kept end, could move a start the opposite way from what was typed); the twin counts a running break as holding everything after its start, as the database does; the empty note is `empty:sr-only`, not `display: none`, so its live region is read as it fills (as access-view's). Tests for the first three; checked on a build, axe clean.
- Next: merge PR4 and this; the plan is done.

## 2026-11-17 · chat · time clock: cancelling a shift and bringing it back on /clock/team/[person] (clock PR4)

- On chat/clock-cancel-screen, stacked on chat/clock-cancel-reads (PR3, #188, not merged yet); the database (PR1, PR2) is on main and migrated. Defaults on Orel's questions, applied until he answers at the end: a shift still open can be cancelled, which ends it (at most a day after it began) so the person can clock in again, and bringing it back opens it again; a cancelled shift comes back any time, by whoever edits the clock, with no reason (the toast's "ביטול" is the same action), refused in words when another shift now covers those hours; the reason is free text, required, up to 200 characters, as a correction's ("למה מבטלים"); a manager who isn't an owner neither cancels nor brings back their own shift, and is told so.
- `@db/time-clock`: `cancelTimeEntry(actor, entry, note)` and `restoreTimeEntry(actor, entry)` with the secret key; a refusal now passes its hint to `clockFailureOf`.
- `@domain/time-clock`: `ClockFailure` gains `cancelled`, `notCancelled` (55000 by their words), `breaksAfterDay` and `notOnStaff` (55000 by their hints); "Only an owner cancels or changes your own hours" stays `ownHours`, "A cancel needs its reason" `reasonNeeded`, P0002 and "Managers and owners only" `noAccess`, 23P01 `times`. `PunchFailure` leaves the four out; `punchFailureOf` reads `cancelled` (taking back a clock-out of a shift a manager cancelled meanwhile) as `notIn`, as it read before, and the other three as `failed`.
- App: `team-types.ts` `EntryActions.cancel(entryId, reason)` and `restore(entryId)`, `EntryTarget` (`add | edit | cancel | cancelled`), and `EntryFailure` with `cancelled`, `notCancelled`, `ownShift`, `overlap`, `breaksAfterDay` and `notOnStaff`. `actions.ts`: `cancelEntryAction` and `restoreEntryAction` behind `z.uuid()`, `editsClock` and `canWrite(viewing)` (one `clockEditor` for every action here now), `parseReason` for the cancel, then `pushSoon()` and `refresh()`; `failureOf` maps `ownHours` to `ownShift` and keeps the cancel's kinds, a restore's `times` is `overlap`, and a confirm or correction of a shift cancelled meanwhile says so. `person-view.tsx`: under "שמירת השעות", a danger-ghost "ביטול המשמרת" (the header's "הוספת משמרת" stays the one main action); its step shows the shift (`ShiftSummary`, the row as the list draws it), "המשמרת נשארת ברשומות, מסומנת כבטלה, ולא נספרת בשעות." (and that it closes one still open), a required "למה מבטלים" and the danger button, with "חזרה לתיקון השעות". On success the sheet closes and "המשמרת בוטלה" shows with "ביטול" for 10 seconds (the documents' pattern: `focusAction`, and `onFocusLeave` back to the row, else the title), which brings it back; if that fails, the cancelled shift opens saying why. A cancelled row now opens on every shape (the whole row on phones and tablets, "החזרה" on desktop): "משמרת שבוטלה" with who, when and why, that it comes back as it was (open again, for one the cancel ended), and "החזרת המשמרת". View-only opens nothing.
- Twins: `demoCancelEntry` and `demoRestoreEntry` (Yoav's evening recorded twice can't come back over the copy left: `overlap`); `/design/shell/clock/team/[person]?state=edit|cancel|cancelled` opens the sheet (`opens` on `PersonHoursView`, after the first paint).
- Messages: `app.clock.history.restore`, `restoreShift`; `app.clock.team.sheet.cancelShift`, `cancel.*` (title, hint, openHint, reason, reasonNeeded, save, back), `cancelled.*` (title, hint, openHint, restore), `done.cancel`, `done.restore`, `done.undo`, and `failure.cancelled`, `notCancelled`, `ownShift`, `overlap`, `breaksAfterDay`, `notOnStaff`.

The desktop "החזרה" button's name didn't start with the word it shows (WCAG 2.5.3, which axe 4.14 checks in the design and team specs): now "החזרה: המשמרת של {day}". ## 2026-11-17 · chat · time clock: a shift's breaks are fixed one at a time, in the database (clock PR2)

- On chat/clock-breaks-db, stacked on chat/clock-cancel-db (clock PR1, #186, not merged yet). Defaults on Orel's questions, applied until he answers at the end: nobody but an owner edits the breaks of their own shift, as for a cancel; a break's times can be changed, a forgotten break added and one pressed by mistake removed, while whether a break is paid stays as it was recorded (a new one follows the setting now) and isn't editable; a removed break is marked and kept, never deleted, and the removal can be taken back for ten minutes by whoever made it, after which the break is added again; a break still running isn't edited (it ends with the person, a clock-out or a correction); in a closed month only an unpaid break's edit tells the closer and the accountants, and nothing is refused for the month being closed.
- Migration `20261117000300_break_edits.sql`: `time_breaks` gains `removed_at` and `removed_by` with two checks (a removed break has ended, so no lookup of an open break meets one); `time_breaks_no_overlap`, same name and still deferrable, counts live breaks only. New `private.check_break_fits` (hint `break_outside`), `private.record_break_edit` (the shift's stamp and the `clock.break_edit` row, `closed_month` only for an unpaid break), and `public.add_time_break`, `change_time_break`, `remove_time_break` (hint `break_open` for a break still running) and `restore_time_break` (hint `too_late`). Each locks the shift, then the break, the order of every other writer of breaks; `end_break` locks only the break and waits for no shift, so nothing can deadlock. Replaced from their latest bodies (each diffed, only `removed_at is null` and comments changed): `private.fit_breaks_to_entry` (hours_kept's: its move, cut and delete touch live breaks only), and PR1's `undo_clock_out` and `restore_time_entry` (reopen live breaks only) and `cancel_time_entry` (`breaks_after_day` asks about live breaks only, so a removed one ending at the cancel's end neither holds it back nor comes back open with a restore); `notify_for_payroll` and its trigger take `clock.break_edit`.
- Tests: `break_edits.test.sql` (64), with removed breaks written straight into the table where no function could leave one, to show a clock-out and an undone clock-out pass them by; time_breaks 43, hours_kept 42, demo_reset 41. `database.types.ts` by hand (no local Docker).
- Next: PR3, the reads (cancelled shifts and removed breaks count nowhere).

## 2026-11-17 · chat · time clock: a wrong shift is cancelled, not deleted, in the database (clock PR1)

- On chat/clock-cancel-db on main (0765017: #185 merged and migrated). The roadmap's last "חובה לפיילוט" row, "ביטול משמרת ועריכת הפסקה ב-/clock/team/[person]", planned in five PRs (database first: cancels, then break edits; then the reads, then the two screens) after a five-reader map of the clock. Defaults on Orel's questions, applied until he answers at the end: an open shift can be cancelled, which ends it then (at most a day after it began) so the person can clock in again, and bringing it back reopens it; a cancelled shift comes back any time, with no reason; the reason is free text, as corrections; the worker isn't notified (their history says it, PR3); nobody but an owner cancels or restores their own shift; the payroll file lists no cancelled shift (a cancel in a closed month is in the next month's differences, with its reason); in a closed month a cancel or a restore always tells the closer and the accountants.
- Migration `20261117000200_cancelled_shifts.sql`: `time_entries` gains `cancelled_at`, `cancelled_by`, `cancel_reason`, `cancelled_open` and `restored_at` with five checks (a cancelled shift always has an end); `time_entries_no_overlap`, same name, counts live shifts only. New `public.cancel_time_entry` and `public.restore_time_entry`. Replaced from their latest bodies (each diffed): `private.notify_for`, `resolve_reminders_for`, `undo_clock_out`, `confirm_time_entry`, `correct_time_entry`, `notify_for_payroll` and `payroll_tick`; the three log triggers take the new actions. The server role keeps its table privileges for now (time_breaks.test.sql writes a break directly as the server).
- Review (3 lenses, each finding verified): every copied body and trigger checked out. Two defects, fixed: a shift left open past its first day ends at the day's end, which cut or deleted a break taken after it and made a restore reopen the wrong one, so such a cancel is refused (55000, hint `breaks_after_day`) until the end is corrected; and a restore reopened a shift for someone who had left the staff (who then can't clock out), now refused (hint `not_on_staff`). A second review found both checks unlocked against a break ended, or a removal from the staff, at the same moment: the cancel now locks the shift's breaks, and the restore holds the membership (for share), so one of the two waits and sees the other. Refuted: a restore leaves the restorer's own confirmation line settled (notify leaves out the actor, as everywhere); the service role's table privileges (kept on purpose, documented).
- Tests: `cancelled_shifts.test.sql` (49); time_clock 51, hours_kept 40, demo_reset 40. `database.types.ts` by hand (no local Docker).
- Next: PR2, break edits in the database.

## 2026-11-17 · chat · lists: a reading back inside its range settles its line

- On chat/lists-range-settles on main (684962a: L6b-2 merged as #184 and migrated). Orel answered the L6 questions at the end of the run: the third, "סגירת ההתראה כשתיקון מחזיר לטווח", yes; the rest as applied (a fix keeps the list closed, one day by default, a replaced photo removed, a named role on workers following workers, the history keeping earlier text, "מי שכבר לא בצוות", the 2-minute fold, a manager out of a department not getting its lines back, pay access by a named role a note only).
- Migration `20261117000100_lists_range_settles.sql`: `private.write_list_item` replaced from L6b-2's body (diffed: two blocks more) settles `list.range:<item>` when a new reading is inside the item's range, as `private.resolve_notifications` does, for set_list_item and fix_list_item alike, and marks it `backInRange: true` in its params. An emptied reading leaves the line; outside again realerts it (`private.notify`) with new params, without the mark. No new log row or key.
- Review (3 lenses, each finding verified against the code): the inbox drew a settled range line exactly as an open one, so for a manager who had read it nothing changed on screen. Now `@domain/notifications` reads the mark (`backInRange`) and the inbox says "הקריאה של {label} ב{name} חזרה לטווח" with no stale reading. The mark, not `settled` alone, decides the words, so a line settled some other way some day doesn't claim the fridge is fine (today nothing else settles these lines: a role change only reads them). The test never went below a minimum: now -3 opens, -1 stays, 0 settles. Lines left open before the migration stay until the next new reading inside the range, as the department lines migration did (refuted as a defect).
- Second review (2 lenses, verified): someone who runs the lists and reads it outside themselves isn't told (private.notify leaves out the actor), so their own settled line kept the mark and said "back in range" while it was outside. Now the out-of-range step takes the mark off every line of the item. And nothing tested the inbox's words: the title moved into `outOfRangeTitle` (`app/(app)/notifications/range-words.ts`), tested in Hebrew and English, settled with and without the mark. Refuted: a settled line keeps who read it outside and when (every kind keeps its actor and time); "של {label}" in the Hebrew title (main already words labels so).
- Third review (2 lenses, verified): once the mark came off the line of whoever read it outside, it never came back, since the settle touched only open lines: that manager's line kept saying "out of range" with an old reading after the fridge was fine. Now the settle marks already settled lines without the mark too, keeping when they settled.
- Fourth review (2 lenses, verified): a first fix for that also gave the reader's own line their new reading, but a line keeps the actor and time of the alert it was written for (private.notify never gives anyone a line for their own reading), so the inbox showed the manager's reading under the worker's name and time, and could push it. Dropped: their line stays the earlier reading with its reader and time, and only loses the mark. The test checks it ("-3 by 3").
- Tests: `lists_range_settles.test.sql` (17); `notifications.test.ts` reads the mark; `range-words.test.ts` the words.
- Next: chat, by #1's order.

- Lists app: `listsAccess` gains `canFix`; `@domain/lists` `fixDeadline(list, {canManage, canFix, holds}, fixHours, now)` mirrors `can_fix_list` (`FIX_HOURS`, `fixWindowHours` as `list_fix_hours` reads the setting), and `currentList` sets `can.fix` and `fixUntil` from it. `@db/list-failures` reads 42501 with hint `fix_window` as "fixOver" ("הזמן לתקן את הרשימה הזאת נגמר. מי שעורך את הרשימות עדיין יכול לתקן אותה."), which ends fix mode and reads the list again, for items and photos alike. The hero says "אפשר לתקן עד 08:05" (`fix.until`, or `fix.untilOn` with the day when it isn't today) while "תיקון הרשימה" is there (not during the finisher's five minutes) and in fix mode; once the window is over, on a fresh load (`fixDeadline`'s `ended`, `ListScreenData.fixEndedAt`) or when a timer from the server's clock reaches it (which also takes "תיקון הרשימה" away), it says `fix.over` ("הזמן לתקן את הרשימה נגמר ב-08:05. מי שעורך את הרשימות עדיין יכול לתקן אותה.", or `fix.overOn` with the day). For someone who can't reopen, the fix card has no "פתיחה מחדש לכולם", and a required item they would empty says `failed.keepRequiredAsk` ("…כדי שיעשו אותו שוב, אפשר לבקש ממי שעורך את הרשימות לפתוח אותה מחדש."); `keepRequired` stays word for word for those who can. `closedWords` gives the day of a close that wasn't today (`card.finishedByOn`, "נסגרה ב-2.11, 08:20 על ידי נועה כהן"), on cards, tables and the hero; a close today reads as before. `/lists` has "עוד אפשר לתקן" (`app.lists.fixable`, "עוד אפשר לתקן # רשימות שנסגרו", with the hint "רשימות מימים קודמים שהגיעו אליך. ליד כל אחת כתוב עד מתי.") after today's closed lists, only when it isn't empty, each card (or table row) with its "אפשר לתקן עד" (`ListsHomeData.fixable` is `{list, until}`): `getMyLists(…, fixableSince)` reads the finished lists a person holds that opened before today and closed within the business's own window (`fixWindowHours` of its settings, read from the member already in hand), and `currentListsHome` checks each with `fixDeadline`, the latest close first; `partsOf` reads the items in pages (`allPages`), so the counts on the cards stay right past the 1000 rows a read returns; cards on a phone and a tablet, a table on a computer. `/manage/lists` draws `fixHours` from the manifest with no screen code; its hint names the switch and the roles screen ("לתפקידים שקיבלו "תיקון רשימות שנסגרו" במסך תפקידים והרשאות. מי שעורך את הרשימות מתקן בכל זמן."), and the switch's hint on the roles screen says where its time is set ("…עד הזמן שבהגדרות הרשימות (יום, אם לא שיניתם)…").
- Twins: `/design/shell/manage/roles?state=senior` (and `&role=senior`): "עובד בכיר" on workers for Noa and Omer, lists at view with the switch on, marked "שונה"; `/design/shell/lists/list-kitchen-opening?state=fix-worker`: Noa's 08:05 close as Omer reads it, "אפשר לתקן עד 4.11, 08:05", his fix his own, no "פתיחה מחדש לכולם"; `/design/shell/lists/worker?state=fixable`: Noa with yesterday's opening under "עוד אפשר לתקן רשימה אחת שנסגרה", "נסגרה ב-2.11, 08:20 על ידי נועה כהן" and "אפשר לתקן עד 08:20". All in design.spec's list, with three tests driving them.
- Tests: unit `fixDeadline` (the window to the millisecond, each offered value and anything else as a day, lists.manage at any time, the switch and the list both needed, open, missed and cancelled never, a finish with no time), `itemFailureOf` ("fixOver", and 42501 without the hint stays "noAccess"), `fixUntilWords`, `fixOverWords`, `closedWords` the next morning and late at night, `fixDeadline`'s `ended`; the roles model at edit with the rule off (lowered and undone, sending alone, reset, a named role at view whose base's lower is undone) and the accepted named-role case; the roles model's (role-access, registry, roles-data, notifications, role-change-words) from the model's part. e2e: `roles.spec` pins three rows when workers are raised (`lists.fix`, `lists.manage`, `lists.send`), three log actors, the undo's three, the toasts unchanged; and Orel's case in a real business (`addRolesSenior`, `rolesClosedList`, `rolesList` in `e2e/db.ts`): "עובד בכיר" given the switch at view (its toast, one rule, the line to its person alone), a plain worker closes a list to every worker with 30°C, the senior fixes it to 3 with "אפשר לתקן עד" and no "פתיחה מחדש לכולם", the close stays the worker's, the worker reads the fix and has no button, off takes the senior's button away, and lists' edit holds it locked with "כלול בעריכה". `lists-fix.spec` (with `setDemoWorkersFix` and `backdateDemoListOpening`): Noa, given the switch, finds yesterday's opening under "עוד אפשר לתקן", is told to ask for a required reading, and fixes it; each card says until when; past the day there is no button, the hero says the time is over and who still fixes it, the section is gone, and the database says the time is over. design.spec's fixable twin checks the close's day and the card's "אפשר לתקן עד".
- Decided here, beyond the plan: "עוד אפשר לתקן" is for those whose fixing has a window (lists.fix without lists.manage), since those who run the lists fix any list at any time and already read the business's day; the window's lists are read by the business's own window, and each is checked again with `fixDeadline`; the hero line shows only while "תיקון הרשימה" is there, and in fix mode; the roles twin's senior role is a state of its own, so the default twin's tests ("שונה" counted on the computer's whole matrix) hold. From the model's part: a switch at view is open and held at view only, and the guard leaves out only unknown switches (an area whose edit or view is missing stays closed). From the database part: `list_fix_hours` compares jsonb, and the hint is raised only after `can_fix_list` fails for someone who holds the key and the list.

- A second review, after the first one's 19 findings were fixed (three lenses, each finding put to a skeptic), confirmed 8 more, all fixed: the backfill as above (it missed a role named on managers when base managers were lowered too, and workers raised before the key, whose named roles could later be handed fixing by their base); the open R10 lines without the key; `role_holds_beyond`, `heldBeyond` and `givesNamedRole` counting running the lists as fixing; a fix refused for want of the right, no hint, now says "כבר אין לך אפשרות לתקן את הרשימה הזאת." (`fixNoAccess`) instead of "הרשימה הזאת כבר לא בשבילך"; the window's timer measures the end on the device's clock once per payload and checks it again when the screen is shown (`visibilitychange`, `pageshow`), so a page brought back keeps no button past the end; `partsOf` in pages and the business's own window for "עוד אפשר לתקן". One was refuted and is left for Orel: someone given the switch who replaces a photo on a closed list removes the earlier one, as for everyone who replaces a photo since L6a.
- Demo, after merging and migrating, in the demo restaurant at 390, 820 and 1440 (its people: Ori the owner, Shiran the manager, Noa of the kitchen and Yoav of the bar, both workers, Galit the accountant). A demo business keeps its people (`55000`, hint `demo`): Ori can add "עובד בכיר" there but can't give it to anyone, so the demo is shown on workers, and the named role on the twin `/design/shell/manage/roles?state=senior` and in the roles spec. Ori opens "תפקידים והרשאות", keeps "רשימות" at view for "עובד/ת" and turns on "תיקון רשימות שנסגרו": the toast says "עובד/ת: עכשיו גם תיקון רשימות שנסגרו", workers' lists say "שונה", and Noa and Yoav each get "מעכשיו גם תיקון רשימות שנסגרו" in the app. Shiran sends a list to "עובדים"; Noa fills it and closes it for both. Yoav opens it (Noa herself has her five minutes first): the hero says "נסגרה ב-… על ידי נועה כהן" and "אפשר לתקן עד …" (with tomorrow's date), he presses "תיקון הרשימה" (no "פתיחה מחדש לכולם" in the card), corrects an item and presses "סיום התיקון": everyone the list is for reads "תוקן אחרי הסגירה · יואב לוי · …", and the list stays Noa's close. Tomorrow, until the hour Noa closed it (a day after the close, `fixHours` 24), the list is under "עוד אפשר לתקן רשימה אחת שנסגרה" on Yoav's and Noa's `/lists`, its card saying the day it closed and until when; after that hour Yoav's hero says "הזמן לתקן את הרשימה נגמר ב-…" and that whoever edits the lists still fixes it, and the section is gone. Raising workers' lists to edit shows the switch on and locked, "כלול בעריכה"; "חזרה להתחלה" puts workers back. Ori can shorten the window on `/manage/lists` ("כמה זמן אפשר לתקן רשימה שנסגרה").
