App: `Viewer` (`role`, `perms`) for `moduleNav`, `moduleWidgets` and `canOpenModule`, a manifest's `nav.outsideStaff` (the time clock's: `clock.payroll` → `/clock/pay`, labelled `modules.time_clock.outsideNav`), so an accountant has two tabs, Home and "שעות לשכר", and no staff screen (`/clock`, `/schedule`, `/clock/team`, `/team`, `/lists`, `/manage` are 404); the session's `perms` come from `my_permissions` (the given ones while it's missing); `@domain/payroll-access` (`canSeePay` by `clock.payroll` alone, `canGrantPay`, `payHolderCount`, the warning past 10, `canGetPersonalPay`, `defaultPayMonth` last month until the 10th, `payMonthFrom`, `decimalHours`) and `@domain/pay-month` (each person's month to the minute: days, shifts, unpaid breaks, corrected, waiting, owner-only, open; each shift's source, correction and its times before the first one), with tests; `@db/payroll`. Screens: `/clock/pay` (the hero with the month and its neighbours, the decimal total and "N אנשים · N משמרות לבדיקה"; one accent card "לפני השכר" when something waits, with the rounding note; phone the people over the hero, each to their month; tablet the list and the chosen person's days, `?person=`; desktop a `DataTable` in a flat card, a person's days in a sheet; "עזב/עזבה" by form of address; an empty month says what comes, or for a month gone by, the way to another; the tablet's chosen person is a `ListRow` with `current`), `/clock/pay/[person]` (hero, summary, the days with a source that names no role ("תיקון ידני", "הוספה ידנית"), who corrected, why and the times before; desktop a days table whose correction cell has the list's own sentence; an empty month by the person's form of address), reached from `/clock/team` and `/clock` for pay holders; each logs its view from `NoteView`, a client part that calls a server action once after mount; the accountant's Home (`accountant-home.tsx`: the month to pay with "פתיחת השעות", what to check before paying (or that the month has no hours yet), and on desktop the three months before; with the clock off, a card that the hours come once it is on, not Today's line about modules); `/manage/access` for owners (a row "מי רואה שכר" on `/manage`; owners and accountants always, managers and workers by switches that save at once and offer undo under them, personal access removed with an undo under its list that holds the write six seconds and writes it at once when the page is hidden or closed or the screen is left (dropping the undo then), adding someone back while their removal is held just lets it go, added from a menu of staff without it, a person whose role already sees pay says so, the count and the warning past 10; read-only in a view, with no menu); activity lines for `permission.grant` / `permission.revoke` (leading to `/manage/access` for the owner) and `payroll.view` routine; the inbox, push words and `@domain/notifications` for `access.pay_granted` (a settled line leads to the inbox: `linkFor(kind, params, settled)` with the kind's `settledHref`, from the inbox, the open route and the inbox's action, `getNotification` reading `resolved_at`). New in @ui: `DataTable` (the first column held at the start while it scrolls sideways; the header scrolls with the page, since the sideways scroller is its scroll container; numbers LTR, rows chosen by a button filling the first cell, the chosen one marked by an accent bar), on `/design` and in CLAUDE.md; `ListRow`'s `current` (the accent bar and `aria-current`), on `/design`. `/design/shell`: `clock/pay` (`?person=`), `clock/pay/[person]`, `manage/access`, `today/accountant` (`?state=empty`, `?state=off`), in `design.spec.ts`. e2e: `payroll.spec.ts` new (Galit's tabs, Home, the seeded month by `seededPayMonth()`, one view a day, Tamar's correction, the 404s; the owner's switch off and undo, with the log, on desktop; the access screen at every size; a manager's way in only while managers have access); `view-as.spec.ts` (as accountant: two tabs and the hours; as owner: no pay, the access screen disabled); the door's spec covers Galit through its people. Deviations from the plan: `canOpenModule` stays false off the staff (the outside screen checks its permission), so `/clock` stays 404 by itself; hours come from a definition's `hours` with fixed cases, not "each staff member" implicitly, and the restaurant uses the fixed pattern (its weeks are the visitor's to build); the corrected shift is `source 'punch'` with `out_proof 'manager'`, as `correct_time_entry` writes it, so it reads "תיקון ידני" and "תוקן על ידי" with the corrector's name; the "without a code" and corrected cases are in the shop only, so the restaurant's clock e2e meets nothing waiting; the line `access.pay_granted` is "קיבלת גישה לשעות ולשכר", with no third-person verb, so no form of address is needed, and in no group (about the reader's own access); a permission row reads as a noun phrase ("מתן גישה לשעות ולשכר למנהלים"), as the log's other lines do; the switches' undo is an `UndoRow` (a status with "ביטול") for six seconds, not a `StatusToast`, which takes no press; the invite form keeps P2's neutral hint for the accountant; `note_payroll_access` takes `pay_month` and `view_kind` (no name a keyword or a column); the console's pay switches wait (the team can switch them through the database functions, which already take platform staff); docs/AGENT.md's payroll line is still left for Orel's session. Checked: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc and the unit and tooling tests (the Windows-only Biome probe aside). Not run here: pgTAP, `supabase db lint`, `pnpm db:types`, `pnpm test:db`, the build, the e2e and the screens at 390, 820 and 1440 px. Next: P3b, one login in several businesses.
- Payroll P4, hourly wages (issue #76), on chat/payroll-wages over chat/payroll-screens (P3, #96), following the payroll plan's §7 with its critique applied, Orel's answers of 9 November and his order of 10 November (managers start without pay access; the wage itself now, no estimate, which is P7 behind a team-level `payEstimate`; one membership per login until P3b) and P1 to P3 as built. `20261110000500_hourly_wages.sql`: `public.minimum_wages` (the minimum wage by the day it took effect, every figure as published: 1.4.2025 34.32 an hour, 1.4.2026 35.40, the statute's 186-hour figure, the youth bands and the apprentice's; read by anyone signed in, written only by migrations; the 2025 youth figures marked `youth_published = false`, since they were worked out from the percentages, not read) and `private.minimum_wage_on(on_day)`; `public.member_wages` (a person's wage in whole agorot from a day, 0.01 to 1,000.00, an optional note of up to 200; append-only, the wage on a day being the latest `effective_from` on or before it, then the latest `set_at`; a `restrict` key to the membership, so a person or business with wages stays (23503); a trigger refuses every update and delete (42501) but a purge that names the business (P1's `private.deleting_allowed`, failing closed) and the setter going to null when their account goes; nobody but its owner writes or truncates it; read by the person while a member, pay holders and a view as an accountant, with the time clock on) and `private.wage_on`; `public.set_hourly_wage(actor, business, person, agorot, from_day, wage_note, at_time)` for the secret key (a pay holder; never their own, 42501; an owner's only by an owner, 42501; an accountant's never, 22023; someone not in the business P0002, one who left allowed; from the first of last month to a year ahead by the business's day, 22023; under the minimum it saves), logging `wage.set` with the wage before on that day, after, the day, the adult minimum of the day and the note; `private.purge_business_records` (P1's) deletes wages before the log; `private.notify_for_payroll` (P3's) tells the person `pay.wage_set` with `since` and no amount, one line per person that a later change brings back unread, and the trigger now takes `wage.set`; `private.seed_demo_pay` (P3's) ends with `private.seed_demo_wages`: a definition's `wages` (`manager` and `worker` in agorot from the first of last month, set by its owner at 08:00 on the day, no log rows or lines; cases `raise`, the case's amount and the role's from the 15th, and `none`), a failing plan writes no wages and the reset stands; both seeds name theirs (45.00 managers, 38.00 workers; the shop's Roni a raise from 36.00, the restaurant's Yoav none), the hosted definitions are patched by id and both hosted demo businesses reset. pgTAP: `wages.test.sql` new (64); `demo_reset` (35: the definition's wages, a visitor's wage gone after the reset, a demo membership deleted after its wage under the setting), `rls_enabled`. Vitest for `pnpm test:db`: `packages/db/minimum-wages.db.test.ts` keeps the database's table equal to the domain's. `database.types.ts` gains both tables and `set_hourly_wage`, by hand in the generator's format. Domain: `@domain/money` (`parseShekels`, `formatShekels`, whole agorot), `@domain/minimum-wage` (`MINIMUM_WAGES`, `minimumWageOn` by band, `minimumWageTableStale` from the April after the last row; tests pin the cut adult hour and the rounded youth hour) and `@domain/wages` (`wageOn`, `wageHistory` with superseded rows, `wagesBetween`, `monthWage`, `wageNow`, `wageFromBounds`, `parseWageForm`, `wageWarning` on the day or on any later April while it stands, `wageFailureOf`; from the April after the newest minimum the sheet says it may be out of date), with tests; `wage.set` in `@domain/activity` and `pay.wage_set` in `@domain/notifications`. `@db/wages` reads wages under the person's session and sets one with the secret key. Screens: `/clock/pay/[person]` has a "שכר לשעה" card (today's wage from its day and the next one set, a warning in words under the minimum, "שינוי השכר לשעה" opening a sheet with the amount, the day (the first of this month unless changed) and a note, under the minimum warning live and saving all the same, the button "שמירת השכר מ-1.11"; the history with who set each and when, a corrected one marked "תוקן"; for one's own "נקבע על ידי מישהו אחר", an owner's for a non-owner and a view said in words, no button), and the month's summary its wages; phone the card under the hero, tablet in the start column, desktop beside the hero; `/clock/pay` lists each person's wage for the month ("36.00 ₪ לשעה · מ-15.10: 38.00 ₪" for a raise), "אין שכר לשעה" or "... לפני" in the warning colour where shifts have none, a desktop column, and the accent card counts them with a link to `?show=noWage`, which lists only them; the accountant's Home has the same row; `/clock` shows the person's own wage and the next one, and nobody else's; the activity log reads `wage.set` (before and after, the note, under the minimum of its day), leading to `/clock/pay`; the inbox and the phone say "השכר לשעה שלך עודכן" with its day, leading to `/clock`. `/design/shell`: the pay demo's wages (Noa's typo fixed and a raise set for December, Yoav's raise in October, Maya none, `?show=noWage`, `clock/pay/yoav`), the clock's own wage, two `wage.set` rows in the activity demo and a line in the inbox demo; `design.spec.ts` visits them and sets a wage in the demo. e2e: `payroll.spec.ts` (the accountant sees the wages and their history; the shop's manager sets Tamar's from the first of the month, the warning at 30.00, the log, their own page with no button, Tamar's `/clock` and her line, Itai's `/clock` with his own and nothing of hers, the rows set put back after; the restaurant's owner sees whose shifts have no wage and lists only them) and `view-as.spec.ts` (as accountant a wage reads and can't be changed); `e2e/db.ts` deletes a membership's wages first under the setting. Deviations from the plan: the minimum wage is also a database table, so the log keeps the minimum of the day; the 2025 youth figures are marked worked out, not confirmed (the plan's critique point 12 is reversed, by the task); `wageFailureOf` lives in `@domain/wages` with tests, and adds `notInTeam`, `notStaff` and `failed`; the warning also looks at later Aprils while a wage stands; the demo's wages are seeded with no log rows, so no lines; activity lines read as noun phrases ("קביעת שכר לשעה לנועה"), as P3's do, so no form of address is needed; no new @ui component (the amount is a `TextField` with `dir="ltr"`, the shekels in its label); the console sets no wages; docs/AGENT.md's payroll line is still left for Orel's session; only another owner sets an owner's wage, so the only owner's is set by nobody here (the plan left it open): an owner's shifts are never counted, listed by `?show=noWage` or warned about as missing a wage, and an owner's own page says the other owners set it or, with none, that it isn't set here (`ownOwner`, `soleOwner`; a known issue in docs/ROADMAP.md); the line says from when and no longer sends the person to the clock, which shows only today's wage and the next, not a back-dated stretch a later wage replaced (also a known issue). Fixed from review: `demo_reset.test.sql`'s two wage assertions were quoted with a single `$`; `wages.test.sql`'s counts of wages, `wage.set` rows and lines now look only at its own businesses, since the seed has wages; the phone's person page shows the month's wage too; the card's warnings say which wage they are about and not "אפשר לשמור גם כך" (`warningNow`, `warningNext`), which only the sheet says; `wageNow` skips a later day with the same amount, so a raise taken back leaves no next; the desktop table's wage column shows a dash, as the list does, where there is nothing to say; the e2e's "אין שכר לשעה" check takes the first, which a tablet shows twice. Checked: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc and the unit and tooling tests (the Windows-only Biome probe aside). Not run here: pgTAP, `supabase db lint`, `pnpm db:types`, `pnpm test:db`, the build, the e2e and the screens at 390, 820 and 1440 px. Next: P5, details for payroll.
- Payroll P5, details for payroll (issue #76), on chat/payroll-details over chat/payroll-wages (P4, #98), following the payroll plan's §8 with its critique applied, Orel's answers of 9 November and his order of 10 November (managers start without pay access; one membership per login until P3b; no estimate before P7), P1 to P4 as built, and the profile design's §2.10: payroll keeps no ID number, address, bank account or birth date, and real ID and bank numbers aren't collected at all yet. `20261110000600_payroll_details.sql`: `public.member_payroll_details` (the payroll system's number, `^[0-9A-Za-z-]{1,20}$` and one person's in a business; the rest day 6, 5 or 0, a day and never a religion; the Hours law `applies`, `trust` for s.30(א)(5) or `unsupervised` for s.30(א)(6); a minor's 18th birthday `youth_until`, null for an adult; `compulsory_education`) and `public.employment_periods` (from, until or open, the last payment once it ended, `joined` for a start copied from the membership and `set` for a holder's, never overlapping: a gist exclusion on the closed day range); both records as hours and wages are (`restrict` keys to the membership, a trigger refuses every delete but a purge that names the business, nobody but the owner writes or truncates them), read like wages (the person while a member, pay holders, a view as an accountant, with the time clock on); `private.backfill_employment_periods()` gave each staff membership one `joined` period from the business's day it began (ended on its last change when inactive) and is safe to run again; `private.track_employment` on memberships (after insert or update of `active`, `role`) opens a `joined` period on the business's day a staff membership starts or comes back, closes the open `joined` one when it stops (inactive, or an accountant now), goes on with the same one when someone is back the day it closed, leaves a holder's `set` period alone, and never fails a membership write over a period; `public.set_payroll_details(actor, business, person, patch, at_time)` for the secret key (only the keys to change, anything else 22023; by the wage's rules: a pay holder, never their own, an owner's only by an owner, never an accountant's; a birth date only from an active owner or the accountant, 42501, a real day in the last 100 years, read once and kept only as `youth_until`, 29 February + 18 years being 28 February; a rest day or Hours law outside the lists is the table's 23514, a number taken 23505; a call that changes nothing writes nothing, and `payroll.details` names the fields that changed, a birth date as `youth_until`, never a value); `public.set_employment_period(actor, business, person, from_day, to_day, paid_until, period_id, at_time)` (adds one, or changes one of the person's, P0002 for another's; by the same rules; an overlap 23P01 'Periods overlap', the end on or after the start, a last payment only once it ended, a start in the last 100 years and at most a year ahead, each said apart; the period is `set` from then on, a `set` one saved as it was writes nothing, and `payroll.period` names the fields that changed, whether it was added and whether a `joined` one was confirmed, never a day); `public.set_hourly_wage` (P4's) refuses a wage before the first period's start once a holder set it (22023 'Before they started'), and its `wage.set` row keeps the minimum of the person's age band on the wage's first day (`private.minimum_wage_for(business, person, on_day)`, under 16, 16 and 17 from `youth_until` as `ageBandOn` reads them, else the adult hour), so a minor's legal wage isn't logged, or read in the activity log, as under the minimum; `public.team_youth(business)` gives owners and managers (and a view as one) who on the staff is a minor today and until when, with the time clock on as for every read of the details, and nobody else anything; the purge (P4's) deletes details and periods before the log; `private.seed_demo_pay` (P4's) ends with `private.seed_demo_details`, from a definition's `details`: payroll numbers from `numbers` in the definition's order for managers and workers, the reset's open periods from `since` (1 March 2026), still `joined`, and the cases `youth` (a minor until the first of the month six months on: 17 through the seeded month), `no_number`, `rest_friday` and `started` (the 10th of three months ago, set by the owner), with no log rows; both seeds name theirs (the shop's Liya a minor with no number yet, Roni resting on Friday, Itai started in the summer; the restaurant numbers only), the hosted definitions are patched by id and both hosted demo businesses reset. pgTAP: `payroll_details.test.sql` new (88: who sets what, the birth date never kept and the log with no number or day, 29 February, an adult, a cleared number, a number taken, periods and their overlap, the trigger on joining from an invite, leaving, coming back after a gap and on the same day, becoming the accountant and a holder's period left alone, a wage before a holder's start, the minimum by age and a minor's wage logged with the minimum of 17, readers, `team_youth` (none with the time clock off), nothing deleting them but the purge, the demo's, the backfill with the trigger off); `demo_reset` (a demo membership deleted after its details and periods under the setting), `hours_kept` (the person who only acted is the business's accountant, who has no employment period to keep their account), `wages` and `lists` (a period purged before an account delete those tests mean to allow), `rls_enabled`. `database.types.ts` gains both tables, `set_payroll_details`, `set_employment_period` and `team_youth`, by hand in the generator's format. Domain: `@domain/youth` (`addYears` as Postgres adds years, `youthUntilOf`, `isMinorOn`, `ageBandOn`, `minorUnderAge`, `youthLabel`), `@domain/typed-date` (three typed numbers to a real day, digits from any keyboard), `@domain/payroll-details` (`REST_DAYS`, `HOURS_LAW`, `detailsOf` with the defaults, `parseDetailsForm`, `detailsFailureOf`, `periodsOf`, `spansOverlap`, `overlapsAny`, `currentPeriod`, `wageFloorOf`, `parsePeriodForm`), with tests; `@domain/wages` gains `wageWarningFor` (the band on the day the wage starts and on each later birthday and change of the table while it stands), `beforeStart` in `parseWageForm` and `wageFailureOf`; `payroll.details` and `payroll.period` in `@domain/activity`. `@db/payroll-details` reads both under the person's session and `team_youth`, and writes both with the secret key. New in @ui: `DateField` (day, month and year typed in three numeric fields, left to right as a date is written, digits only, moving on when one is full, real labels in a fieldset), on `/design`. Screens: `/clock/pay/[person]` has a "פרטים לשכר" card (the number or "חסר" in the warning colour, the rest day, the Hours law, "נוער עד 4.2027" and school for a minor, and each period, today's first, "עובד/ת מ-1.3.2026" by the form of address, with "לפי ההצטרפות לאפליקציה, כדאי לבדוק" for a `joined` one and the last payment when there is one); for those who may, "עריכת הפרטים" opens a sheet (the number, the rest day with "רק אם … ביקש/ה" by the form of address, the Hours law with the section and, under management or personal trust, that courts give it only to the most senior posts and the law then doesn't apply at all, rest-day pay included; for an owner or the accountant "תאריך לידה, אם עדיין לא מלאו 18" in a `DateField` with "לא נשמר. נשמר רק עד מתי זה נוער." and what the date makes of the age before saving; school for a minor; "שמירת הפרטים"), a period row or "הוספת תקופת עבודה" opens one with its start, end and last payment (`DateField`s, the joined note, "שמירת התקופה", an overlap said in words before the database is asked); one's own, an owner's for a non-owner and a view say why in words with no buttons; phone the card under the wage card, tablet in the start column, desktop under the wage in the summary row; the wage card warns by the person's age and each later birthday, starts the day field from a later start, and refuses a day before a holder's start in the sheet; `/clock/pay` marks a minor with "נוער" (the row's one badge, and in the desktop table's name cell), the desktop table has "מספר עובד", and the accent card and the accountant's Home say "חסר מספר עובד ל-…" for staff (not owners) with shifts and no number; `/clock` shows the person's own details (number, rest day, a minor's "until" and a start a holder set) and nobody else's; `/team/[person]` shows a manager "נוער עד" from `team_youth` and nothing else of the details; the activity log reads `payroll.details` ("עדכון מספר העובד וגיל הנוער של ליה כץ", the fields as a list) and `payroll.period` (added, changed by field, or confirmed as copied from joining), leading to `/clock/pay`. `/design/shell`: the pay demo's details (Noa's start copied from joining, Yoav resting on Friday and back after an earlier period, Maya a minor with no number, the accent card's count), the clock's own details, `team/maya` with her "until", two payroll rows in the activity demo, the accountant Home's count; `design.spec.ts` visits `team/maya` and enters a birth date and an overlapping period in the demo. e2e: `payroll.spec.ts` (at every size, the minor's badge, Itai's set start and number, Tamar's start to check, Roni's Friday; on desktop, Galit enters Liya's number and birth date, only the 18th birthday kept, the log by field with no value, the count gone, the owner reading the log, put back after; the owner sets Roni's start, the period becomes the owner's, the log with no day, a wage from before it refused in the sheet, put back after), `view-as.spec.ts` (as accountant the details read and can't be changed); `e2e/db.ts` deletes a membership's details and periods first under the setting, and reads and puts back details and periods. Deviations from the plan: the backfill is a function the migration runs once, so pgTAP can run it again with the trigger off; a `joined` start limits no wage (a business joining today still sets last month's wages: only a holder's start does), and the trigger reopens a period closed the same day instead of opening a second one, and never fails a membership write; `set_employment_period` takes `period_id` (not `period`, a keyword in newer Postgres) and an `at_time`, and refuses a start more than a year ahead; details and periods follow the wage's rules for owners (only an owner sets an owner's) and are written only with the time clock on; a birth date of null clears the youth (from the same circle); `team_youth` is new, so managers without pay access see a minor's "until" (on `/team/[person]`) and nothing else, as the plan's table asks; the worker's own view is on `/clock`, and leaves out a start copied from joining; `parsePeriodForm` and `parseDetailsForm` read `DateField`'s three numbers, and the wage's date stays a date input as P4 built it; no notification for details (the plan has none); activity lines are noun phrases, as P3's and P4's are; CLAUDE.md's "Built so far" doesn't list `DateField` yet (not changed in this run), nor docs/AGENT.md's payroll line, still left for Orel's session. Fixed from review: `team_youth` now asks for the time clock, as the details' policy does; the wage log's minimum follows the person's age (above); `set_employment_period` says a start more than 100 years back in its own words ('A period starts in the last 100 years'); `database.types.ts` writes the details' key to the membership as `isOneToOne: true`, since its columns are the table's primary key; on phone and tablet the month's list says "חסר מספר עובד" beside each person the count counts (`PayPerson.noNumber`, the same rule as the count), the accent card links to `?show=noNumber`, which lists only them, and the accountant's Home row leads there too, with the design twin and a `design.spec.ts` visit; the payroll number's hint and error say digits, Latin letters and dashes, and `parseDetailsForm` reads the number through `cleanTyped`, so a maqaf, an en dash or Arabic-Indic digits are read as "-" and 0-9; `DateField` keeps Arabic-Indic, Persian and full-width digits (`cleanTyped` before the digits are kept); the details sheet reads the birth date apart from the rest (`parseBirth`, now also used by `parseDetailsForm`), so a wrong number neither hides what the date makes of the age nor moves the school switch; the period sheet says that a start copied from joining can be confirmed as it is, and its button reads "אישור תאריך ההתחלה" while the form still matches the stored period, "שמירת התקופה" once anything changes; the period sheet moves the focus to the first wrong field, for the server's errors too; the accountant's e2e types a birth date worked out from the shop's month (17 years before the 15th of the month after next), not 15.4.2009, which would have turned adult on 15 April 2027. Checked: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc and the unit and tooling tests (the Windows-only Biome probe aside); by reading, every changed SQL file's dollar quotes, each pgTAP plan against its assertions, every call's arguments and every column read. Not run here (no database, no CI): pgTAP, `supabase db lint`, `pnpm db:types`, `pnpm test:db`, the build, the e2e and the screens at 390, 820 and 1440 px. Next: P6, the legal hours engine.
- Payroll P6, the legal hours engine (issue #76), on chat/payroll-engine over chat/payroll-details (P5, #100), following the payroll plan's §9 and §13 with its critique applied, Orel's answers of 9 November (rest minutes past 42 a week at 175%/200%, each flagged to check) and his order of 10 November (the estimate behind a team-level `payEstimate`, off by default and on in the demo; no new screens), and P4 and P5 as built. `20261110000700_pay_rules.sql`: `public.set_module_settings` (sealed's, replaced) keeps the punch code for an owner or the team with its own words, adds the pay rules (`workWeek`, `shortDay`, `restStart`, `restStartTime`, `workersSeeEstimate`: 42501 'Only an owner or the team changes the pay rules') and the estimate for the team alone (`payEstimate`: 42501 'Only the team switches the pay estimate'), each checked for its type (22023 'Not a pay rule setting'); `private.seed_demo_pay` (P5's) first lays a demo definition's `settings` (by module key) over its template's, so both demo businesses show the estimate; the hosted definitions are patched by id and their settings set at once, with no reset. pgTAP: `pay_rules.test.sql` new (31). `database.types.ts` unchanged (no signature changed). Domain: `@domain/payroll` (`payrollMonth`, `personPayroll`, `payrollRange`, `bucketAgorot`, `hoursText`, `withoutAmounts`, `ownEstimateShown`, `payrollPersonOf`, `payrollPeopleOf`, `payrollReadRange`): workdays by the local date of the clock-in, paid time to the millisecond with unpaid breaks off and open entries counting nothing, each day's norm (six: 480, 420 on the day before the rest day and the eve of a holiday the person doesn't work; five: 516, 456 on the short day, 420 on those eves; 420 for a night shift of 120 paid minutes between 22:00 and 06:00 counted on instants), the day walked in time order to regular, 125%, 150% and in a rest window 150%, 175%, 200%, then the week's regular and rest-150% time toward 2,520 minutes with a paid holiday or election day not worked counted at its average, the rest's past it flagged `restWeekOvertime`; an `unset` week worked out as five, as six and by each day's smaller norm, the highest Σ minutes × percent kept (ties to six) and noted `workWeekUnset`; s.30 people all regular with `hoursLawRestDay`; a shift after a holiday within 36 hours of the end of the person's last work before it, holiday work as a whole (`holidayNight`); minutes by largest remainder so a day's rates add up to the clock's minutes; wage segments by `wageOn`, amounts rounded per rate and segment, the estimate null while a worked day has no wage; the flags of §9.5 and the month's notes. `@domain/payroll-calendar` (hebcal, server only): the nine paid holidays by Hebrew date and Independence Day from hebcal, each day's window (candle lighting on the eve to havdalah, Rosh Hashana's second day from the first's end, Independence Day 20:00 to 20:00), the eves, the weekly rest by rest day (Saturday from candle lighting or a fixed Friday time for 36 hours and at least until havdalah; Friday and Sunday rests 18:00 to 06:00), election day 2026 07:00–22:00. `@domain/holiday-pay` (`holidayPayLines`, `payrollHistory`, `wouldHaveWorked`, `addMonthsToDay`, `historyMonths`). `@domain/payroll-flags` (the flag and note types, `PAYROLL_FLAGS` by group, the statute's `LIMITS`, the adults' and minors' checks). `@domain/payroll-readings`: every §14 reading an accountant or lawyer must confirm is one field of `PAYROLL_READINGS`, so a correction is one change. `@domain/money` gains `minutesAgorot`, and `wageOn` in `@domain/wages` reads any row with `from` and `setAt`. Every §13 case is a vitest case in `payroll.test.ts` (C1–C30 with C4f, C4u, C7n, C8f, C10s, C16r, C17b, C17c (a), (b) and with no work in the week before, C17d, C19s, C16r, C25 and C28 in both readings, C26b, C28, C29, all passing), with `payroll-calendar.test.ts`, `holiday-pay.test.ts` and `payroll-flags.test.ts`. The time clock's manifest has the six settings with `.meta()` (`payEstimate` at team level, default off; the rest at owner level), their words under `modules.time_clock.settings`, and the engine's words for P7 (`modules.time_clock.payroll.flags`, `.notes`, `.holidays`, `.oneOffRestDays`), checked by `tooling/payroll-words.test.ts`; `tooling/demo-pay-estimate.test.ts` keeps the estimate on in both seeds' demo definitions and in no template. Loaders for P7: `currentPayrollMonth(month)` in `app/(app)/clock/pay/pay-month.ts` (everyone's month for pay holders, every amount dropped while `payEstimate` is off) and `currentOwnPay(month)` in `app/(app)/clock/own-pay.ts` (the person's own month under their own session, null unless `payEstimate`, `workersSeeEstimate` and a wage). `e2e/clock-code.spec.ts` takes the first owner-only mark, since the pay rules carry it too. Deviations from the plan: a fifth domain file for the readings, and `PayrollInput.readings` so C25's other reading is a test (screens never pass it); `shortDay` is a choice of Sunday to Thursday, not the weekday editor, which offers all seven; the pgTAP is its own file; a holiday counts as worked when a paid minute of the person's falls in its window, except that a shift that clocked in before the holiday began counts only by its minutes on the holiday's own date (`eveShiftFromHolidayDate`, §14 accountant 4 and lawyer 2), so the eve's shift past candle lighting keeps the 7-hour eve and the holiday-pay line (C17b), while a shift wholly in the holiday's evening (Yom Kippur's, or Rosh Hashana's second day from Saturday's havdalah) or a night shift from the eve into the day works it: no pay line, no 7-hour eve, and `holidayWorked` with those minutes; the eves leave out a day that is itself a holiday; a paid day not worked counts toward the 42 from its local midnight; `wasScheduled` is added only when the history doesn't show the weekday (C29 over §9.4's fixed list), with `noHistory` and no amount when there is no history; the history's `weeks` are the weeks the person worked, worked out in the domain from three months of entries (no `getMonthTotals`); holiday and election-day lines need an employment period covering the day; ties in `unset` go to six, then five; the weekly rest is the longest stretch without work that starts in the week, to the next work, and a Sunday rester's week runs Saturday 18:00 to Saturday 18:00; `belowMinimumWage` runs over the month's days with a wage, by age on each; adults' hour limits skip s.30 people and a minor's days, and a minor's 9-hour day needs the business's week set to five; `nightWeekOver58` counts the week's hours when it has a night shift; the loader is `currentPayrollMonth`, since P3's `currentPayMonth` already reads the hours list, and it works out the month for the whole staff, owners included (an owner without a wage gets `noWage` and no estimate: P7 decides how an owner shows, as P4 did for the list); `currentOwnPay` is null while `payEstimate` is off too. After review: a shift that starts in a holiday and runs past its end within the 36 hours is holiday work as a whole too (`holidayNight`), so coming in before havdalah never pays less than after; `holidayWorked` carries `paidAt` (`regular` for s.30 people, whose words then say the hours were counted as regular and that holiday pay may still be owed, with no 150% or 250%); the week someone turns 18 is checked by age on each day (the minor's 40 hours over the days before the birthday, the adults' limits over the days from it); every §14 reading now drives the engine in one place: `hoursLawRestRegular` (the other reading pays s.30 rest minutes 150%), `paidAbsenceCountsAt` (`average` or `norm`), the new `holidaysForOtherRestDays` (read by `holidaysFor` in `@domain/holiday-pay` for the engine and the pay lines) and `eveShiftFromHolidayDate`, each with a test of its other value; `getTimeEntries` reads page by page (`allPages` in `packages/db/pages.ts`, ordered by `clock_in` then `id`), since PostgREST stops at 1000 rows without an error and payroll reads a whole business over four months, with unit tests past 1000 rows; `dateIn` keeps one date formatter per time zone. Checked: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc and the unit and tooling tests (the Windows-only Biome probe aside); by reading, the migration's and the pgTAP's dollar quotes, `plan(31)` against its assertions, every call's arguments and the columns read, and that the punch code's messages `own_hours.test.sql` asserts are unchanged. Not run here (no database, no CI): pgTAP, `supabase db lint`, `pnpm db:types`, `pnpm test:db`, the build, the e2e and the screens. Next: P7, the rates and the estimate on screen.
- Payroll P7, the rates and the estimate on screen (issue #76), on chat/payroll-estimate over chat/payroll-engine (P6, #101), following the payroll plan's §10 with its critique applied, Orel's answers of 9 November (every amount "הערכה לפני מס"; workers see their own month's estimate; rest minutes past 42 "לבדיקה"; managers without pay access see no amounts) and his order of 10 November (amounts only while the team's `payEstimate` is on, so a real business reads hours and rates without a shekel until an accountant confirms), and P3 to P6 as built. No SQL: no migration, `database.types.ts` unchanged. Domain: `@domain/pay-rates` (`personRates`: a person's month by rate, by wage with each stretch's workdays, nights and breaks, and by day with its split, kind and remarks; `remarksOf`, what a pay holder checks, minors first then by day, without the flags the screens already say in their own words (open, corrected, waiting, no wage, no number) and with the two s.30 flags apart as how someone is paid (`aboutPayOf`); `toCheckOf`, the remarks and every line of pay for a day not worked; `remarkLines` for the month's card, one line per person and kind with how many more, minors first; `flagValues`, the values of each flag's words from the screen's formatters; `keyedFlags`; `monthEstimate`, the known estimates added and those left out for want of a wage counted; `weeksWorkedOut`; `restNameOf`/`restNameOfAll` naming the rest rates by rest day; `ratesShown`, `daySplit`, `dayTags`; `ownMonthOf`, the worker's own month with their five adult limits and only whether a minor's rule was crossed), with tests; `tooling/payroll-words.test.ts` now holds every flag's words to the values `flagValues` passes, and checks the words of every rate, holiday-pay check and worker's limit. Loaders: `payAccess` moved to `clock/pay/pay-access.ts` (the month's loaders and the engine's read it without importing each other); `pay-month.ts` shares `payrollInputOf` and `payrollReadOf` and adds `currentPayrollPerson` (one person's reads, with the month's notes); `currentPayMonth`, `currentPayPerson` and `currentPayHome` (the accountant's Home, from the same `currentPayrollMonth`) add the engine's month, `payEstimate` and, while it is on and the month has hours, the estimate; `currentOwnPay` returns the clock's `OwnPay` (with the rest day, the business and the person's form of address) and is read by `/clock` and `/clock/history`'s pages. Screens: `/clock/pay` says under its title "הערכה לפני מס, לפי השעות. בלי ניכויים, חופשה, מחלה ונסיעות." or, with amounts off, that the estimate isn't on for the business yet and the hours by rate are here; the hero adds the month's estimate (and who it leaves out for want of a wage) and "N הערות לבדיקה"; the accent card "לפני השכר" adds the engine's remarks in P6's words, each with whose it is and "ועוד N פעמים", at most five, then how many more, and follows `?show=`; phone rows add "1,234.50 ₪ הערכה" and "N הערות לבדיקה"; the tablet's person column shows the month by rate and each shift with its day's split; the desktop table has every rate (the rest columns named by the rest day everyone shares, else for any rest day), night shifts, the wage, the estimate while amounts are on, and the phone row's counts, each its own column ("תוקנו", "משמרות לבדיקה" for those waiting or open, "הערות לבדיקה" for the engine's), and its sheet the month by rate. `/clock/pay/[person]`: "לפי תעריף" (each rate with minutes and, with amounts, what it comes to, a heading row per wage after a raise, then the pay for a holiday or election day not worked with its average and what to check by the person's form of address, then "הערכה לפני מס" or why there is none) with "איך זה מחושב", a sheet with the method in plain words (and for a pay holder that some rules wait for an accountant and how each week of an unset work week was read); "הערות לבדיקה" in words, with the unset work week's note and, for an owner, the way to the clock's settings, the stale minimum wage and the paid absences the app doesn't know; each shift on its day's last line "מתוכן 0.60 נוספות 125%", the kind of day (a holiday or election day by name, Shabbat or the rest day, an eve, a night shift) and its remarks in the warning colour; the desktop days table adds every rate, the kind of day and what to check; night shifts in the hero's line and the summary. `/clock`: the person's own month in shekels, a flat card under their wage ("השכר שלך ב…", the estimate with "הערכה לפני מס", the hours at each rate, holiday and election-day pay with "אם עבדת יום לפני ויום אחרי" / "אם היית אמור/ה לעבוד בו" by their form of address, their adult limits in neutral words, for a minor "כדאי לדבר עם {business}", the wage it used, what it leaves out and that the payslip decides, and the method sheet without the accountant's part); `/clock/history` the same card for the month shown; nothing while `payEstimate` or `workersSeeEstimate` is off or without a wage. The accountant's Home adds the estimate under the hero and "N הערות לבדיקה בשעות" to what to check. Messages under `app.pay.rates`, `rateColumns`, `estimate`, `rateCard`, `holidayPay`, `remarks`, `notes`, `tags`, `method`, `day`, `columns`, `dayColumns`, `app.clock.ownPay` and `app.home.accountant.remarks`, he and en. `/design/shell`: the pay demo runs the real engine over its October (with July to September for holiday pay; Tel Aviv, the work week unset): Yoav's election day paid with what to check, Shmini Atzeret on his day, his night inside his Friday rest, Noa and Shiran working on election day, Maya's short breaks as a minor; `?estimate=off` on `clock/pay`, `clock/pay/[person]`, `today/accountant`, `clock` and `clock/history`; the clock demo gains a Friday past candle lighting and Noa's own November. `design.spec.ts` visits both states and opens the method sheets; `payroll.spec.ts` adds the accountant's month by rate with Liya's evening past 20:00 in words, the desktop's rate columns, Tamar's 175% and Itai's 150%, the method sheet; the restaurant's Yoav's night at 125% and no estimate without a wage; Tamar's own estimate on her clock and history and nothing of hers on Itai's, none with `workersSeeEstimate` or `payEstimate` off while pay holders keep the rates (`e2e/db.ts` `shopClockSettings`, `setShopClockSettings`). Deviations from the plan: the worker's card is flat on `/clock` as the plan has it, but because the punch circle is that screen's hero surface (`hero-surface`), not a month hero, which `/clock` doesn't have (on the history the month is the hero); the desktop table keeps one row per person, so every column is that person's month, and a raise splits by wage on the sheet and the page; the table drops P3's shifts and waiting columns for the rates (the summary keeps them, and "משמרות לבדיקה" counts the waiting with the open); the card shows only with a wage (P6's `currentOwnPay`), so "השכר לשעה עוד לא נקבע" becomes the line for a month partly without one; the worker sees five adult limits and one line for minors, nothing else; an unset work week is one note with the way to the settings, each week's way in the method sheet; the month's card names remarks by person and kind in P6's neutral words, prefixed with the name, instead of sentences like "ליה עבדה אחרי 20:00"; holiday-pay lines count as things to check, and are listed where they are counted (one line per person on the month's card, each line with what to check on it in a person's "הערות לבדיקה"); a month with no hours has no estimate; the desktop days table has a kind-of-day column; no new @ui component, so CLAUDE.md is unchanged (`DateField` is still not in its list); `e2e/screens.spec.ts` is unchanged, since it sweeps /design pages: the pay screens' axe and overflow checks are in `payroll.spec.ts` at every size and their twins in `design.spec.ts`; no activity line or notification, as the plan has none. After review: the worker's card is flat on `/clock`, the punch circle staying the screen's one hero, and the method button lost its on-hero look; a month has an estimate with minutes at a rate or a line of pay for a day not worked (`hasEstimate` in `@domain/pay-rates`), so a month with only holiday or election-day pay shows its lines and estimate on the worker's card as on the pay holder's page, a listed person with neither shows no "0.00 ₪" (the phone row, the table's cell, the estimate row), and the history leaves the card out of a month with nothing to estimate and no shift open, its empty state being the only one; a shift still open is said apart, since the month's hours count it up to now and the engine once it ends: "משמרת אחת עוד פתוחה, ותיספר בתעריפים כשתסתיים" before payroll, on Home and under a person's rates (`PersonRates.open`), and "המשמרת שעוד פתוחה תיספר כאן כשתסתיים." on the worker's card (`OwnMonth.open`); the hero's line says shifts and remarks each on its own and "אין מה לבדוק" only with neither (`payCaption`, `app.pay.captionParts`, on `/clock/pay` and the accountant's Home); `remarkLines` gives a person's pay for days not worked one line, so the month card's lines and "ועוד N" add up to its count, and a person's "הערות לבדיקה" lists each line with its checks; a person's night shifts are in the hero's line, which every shape shows; a `DataTable` that only reads is a region named by its caption that takes the keyboard (axe `scrollable-region-focusable`, for the person's wide days table at 1440), with a wide read-only table on /design; the unused `payAmountsShown`, `rateCard.span` and `rateCard.nights` are gone; P6's per-week `modules.time_clock.payroll.notes.workWeekUnset` and `roundingIgnored` stay, with their test, for P8's file, while the screens say the unset week once (`app.pay.notes.workWeekUnset`, each week a row in the method sheet) and the rounding in `beforePayroll.rounding`, so the two pairs must be changed together; the clock demo's month hours are added from its shifts and the running one, `?state=working` shows the card's open-shift line, the pay demo has Shiran's shift left open on the 29th (24 hours in the hours, none in the rates), and `/design/shell/today/accountant?state=checked` is a month with every shift approved and closed and remarks to check, all four checked in `design.spec.ts`; the restaurant's Yoav e2e reads the month's 125% and a day's split as patterns, since each Thursday is also the five-day week's short day (24 minutes at 125%). Checked: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc, and vitest over packages, modules and tooling (the Windows-only Biome probe aside). Not run here (no database, no CI): the build, the e2e (`payroll`, `design`, and the clock, clock-team and view-as specs the new card and columns reach) and the screens at 390, 820 and 1440 px. Next: P3b, one login in several businesses.
- Payroll P8, the file for payroll (issue #76), on chat/payroll-export over chat/payroll-estimate (P7, #102), following the payroll plan's §11 and §3 with its critique applied, Orel's answers of 9 November (no estimate and no amount worked out anywhere in the file, not even on a summary sheet; a generic Hebrew XLSX, not a Hilan or Michpal layout) and his order of 10 November (P8 before P3b: one membership per login, no switcher assumed), and P4 to P7 as built. No new dependency: the XLSX and its ZIP are written here. `20261110000800_payroll_export.sql`: `public.note_payroll_export(business, pay_month, person_count, shift_count, engine_version)` (the person's own session; a `clock.payroll` holder or a view as an accountant, with the time clock on, else 42501; 22023 for no month, a count under zero or none, or no engine version; every file one `payroll.export` row, `{month, format: "xlsx", people, shifts, ids: false, engine}`, a pay row only pay holders read; executable by `authenticated`, not `anon`). pgTAP: `payroll_export.test.sql` new (26: who may call it, what a file must say, the owner twice, the accountant, a view as an accountant with the staff actor, a worker, a manager without and then with pay access, a view as a manager, another business's owner, the clock off and signed out refused, and who reads the rows). `database.types.ts` gains `note_payroll_export`, by hand in the generator's format. Domain: `@domain/payroll-export` (pure): `exportSheets(month, people, meta, words)` gives the four sheets of §11.3 in the business's words, right to left: "סיכום חודשי" a row per person per stretch at one wage that holds a day worked, a day not worked and paid, or a shift (a raise is two rows; a raise after the last day worked, with election day after it, is a row of its own at the new wage, and a month with only a day not worked still shows the wage in force; each shift, day not worked and thing to check on the row of the wage on its day; the stretches are `monthWageSpans` in `@domain/payroll`, which the engine's `byWage` now reads too, from the wages the loader passes with each person), with the wage as set, the stretch, workdays, paid hours, the six rates, night shifts, unpaid break hours, shifts corrected, open (not counted) and waiting, the Hours law, the holidays and election day not worked with their date and average (never an amount) and how many things to check; "פירוט יומי" a row per shift, with its times, those before a correction, paid and unpaid break minutes (each break to the minute, as the engine counts them), net hours (exact, shown to two places, so a column adds up to the summary, which rounds summed minutes once), and on the day's first counted shift only its rates and what kind of day it was (night, eve, holiday or rest, election day: the day's, so a morning shift isn't marked as a later shift's night or Shabbat), how it was recorded (`off` "שעון, העסק לא מבקש קוד" apart from a punch), whether it is open or waiting, who corrected it, when and why; "לבדיקה" a row per thing to check in P6's words, with its rule and section (`app.pay.file.rules`) and "השעות לא שונו" (the s.30 flags, the remarks minors first, no wage, no payroll number, each day not worked with what to check by the person's form of address, and each week of an unset work week in P6's per-week words); "על הקובץ" the business, the month, when and by whom (the team's view said so), the engine version, the minimum wage table's date, the settings, the month's notes and six lines (minutes exact, the split a proposal, the daily hours kept unrounded, a day's rates and marks on its first shift, open shifts not counted, days not worked without an amount, no estimate, no amount and no ID numbers). It never reads an estimate, a segment's amount or a day's agorot, so the file is the same with `payEstimate` on or off (a test holds it), and the route drops the amounts as well. Also `exportShiftsOf`, `escapeCellText` (an apostrophe before `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, a tab, a carriage return or their full-width forms; safe twice), `changedSinceExport` (a shift of the month written, corrected, confirmed or ended after the last file, or a wage set since that reaches into it), `exportFileName`, `cleanFileName`, `attachmentHeader`, `localDateTime`, `entriesOfMonth`, with `payroll-export.test.ts` (23). Server code in `app/(app)/clock/pay/export/`: `zip.ts` (local headers, the central directory and its end record, UTF-8 names, CRC-32 by table, each entry deflated with node:zlib's `deflateRawSync`, no ZIP64, the same bytes for the same entries), `xlsx.ts` (SpreadsheetML: [Content_Types].xml, the package's and the workbook's relationships, `workbook.xml`, a sheet each with `rightToLeft="1"`, the first row frozen and the columns' widths, `styles.xml` with 0.00, 0, dd/mm/yyyy, hh:mm and dd/mm/yyyy hh:mm, shared strings; text never a formula, XML escaped and what XML can't hold left out, sheet names as Excel takes them), `export-words.ts` (the file's words from the messages, as plain text), `file.ts` (`payrollFileOf`, shared with the demo, and the response: `Content-Disposition: attachment` with an ASCII `filename` and a UTF-8 `filename*`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, `nosniff`), `export-data.ts` (`currentPayrollFile`: the person's own session, `payAccess` first, a real month up to this one, the reads `currentPayrollReads` shares with the screens), `route.ts` (`GET /clock/pay/export?month=YYYY-MM`, nothing else in the address; a prefetch gets 204, another site's link 404, and a HEAD 405, since Next would answer it with GET and build and log a file that never goes out; 404 for anyone without pay access and for a month that isn't one; `notify_for_payroll` (P4's, copied) tells each such month's closer and the accountants `pay.after_close` (dedupe `pay.after_close:<month>`, back unread with each later change) and settles `pay.month_due` on `payroll.close`, its trigger now `permission.grant`, `permission.revoke`, `wage.set`, `payroll.close` and only the clock rows that carry `closed_month` or `closed_from_month`; `private.payroll_tick(at_time)` and the pg_cron job `payroll` (`0 6 * * *` UTC; `seed.sql` switches it off with the notifications' two): on a business's own 2nd, with the time clock on, not a demo, and last month holding hours and not closed, its pay holders get `pay.month_due` with how many shifts wait for a confirmation and are open (dedupe `payroll.month:<month>`, never told twice); `purge_business_records` (P5's) deletes the months before the log. pgTAP: `payroll_months.test.sql` new (60: grants and the job; the reminder on the 3rd and the 2nd with its counts, once, none in a demo, a manager given pay access told as a pay holder; an open shift counted, a business with no hours told nothing, a month that ends by the business's clock and not UTC's (23:30 and 00:30 in Israel), and a month closed before its tick never told; a manager, a manager given pay access, a worker and the team refused, the current month and no snapshot refused, the accountant closing, the row and its log, the reminder settled, closing twice; a wage into the month refused and one from the next month saved; a correction and an added shift going through with `closed_month`, one line for the accountant who closed it, none for the owner or the manager, a November correction naming none, a shift moved out of the closed month naming it, and one moved from one closed month into another naming both, with a line to each month's closer and the accountant; reopening without a reason or with a blank one, by a manager or one given pay access, then by the owner with the row and the log keeping the reason, twice refused, a wage then saved and a correction naming no month, closed again anew; who reads the months; nothing deletes one but the purge); `rls_enabled.test.sql` names the table. `database.types.ts` gains `payroll_months`, `close_payroll_month` and `reopen_payroll_month`, by hand in the generator's format. Domain: `@domain/payroll-diff` (`closedSnapshot`: the engine's month without amounts, the pay rules alone, the business's place (its city, a missing one as Tel Aviv, and time zone) and the people as the engine read them; `readSnapshot`, by Zod, null for anything else; `closedPeople`; `closedReads`, what a closed month is worked out again with (its own place, a snapshot without one taking the place now); `monthDifferences(closed, now)`, per person, per day and per rate, minutes only, with the shifts behind each day; `changedDays`), `@domain/payroll-close` (`closesMonths`, `monthEnded`, `closeFailureOf`), `@domain/wages` (`parseWageForm` refuses a day of a closed month, `firstOpenDay`, `monthClosed` for 55000), `@domain/payroll-export` (a fifth sheet "הפרשים" before "על הקובץ", `DIFF_COLUMNS`: name, number, the month it belongs to, date, weekday, hours then and now, the difference in all and at each rate, why; still no amount; the sheet about the file says when the month before closed, and whether it or the engine changed since), `@domain/notifications` (`pay.after_close`; `pay.month_due`, which needs action; both lead to that month's `/clock/pay?month=`), `@domain/activity` (`payroll.close`, `payroll.reopen` with its reason), each with tests (`payroll-diff.test.ts` new, with plan §12.2's 30 minutes more on a 9-hour day as `ot125 +30`, and the path the screen takes, through JSON and `readSnapshot`: no difference after a new wage and a move to Jerusalem, a correction the only one, and Friday's minutes moving between rates if the city now leaked in; `payroll-close.test.ts` new). Deviations from the plan: the migration is `…001000`; the kinds are `pay.after_close` and `pay.month_due` (§12.1 says `payroll.month_due`, but §12.2's words are `kinds.pay.month_due`, and a line's words follow its kind, as `pay.wage_set`'s), with §12.1's dedupe keys; the trigger keeps `permission.revoke` (§12.1's list leaves it out, which would stop settling access taken back); the activity words are `actions.payroll.close` and `reopen` beside P5's and P8's `actions.payroll.*`, not `payroll_close`; demo businesses get no reminder at the reset (§12.1 says they get it in the app there): the task's rule is never for a demo business, P5 left `seed_demo_pay` alone for P9, and a line that needs action after every reset would put a count on every demo pay holder's Home that no spec expects; the reminder skips a month with no hours; the snapshot keeps no amount (they follow from the minutes and wages it keeps), and a closed month is worked out again with its own settings and people, so a later change of settings or details makes no difference, while a new engine version may, and the file says so; "הפרשים" holds the month just before the file's, not every closed month before it, so no difference is in two files; the e2e makes its correction through the database function rather than `/clock/team`. Checked: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc, and vitest over packages, modules, app and tooling (the Windows-only Biome probe aside); by reading, the migration's and the pgTAP's dollar quotes, `plan(60)` against its assertions, every copied function against its newest definition (`set_hourly_wage` P5's, `add_time_entry` and `correct_time_entry` P1's, `notify_for_payroll` and its trigger P4's, `purge_business_records` P5's; `private.notify`'s and `private.resolve_notifications`' arguments), the typed literals, and that no parameter is named like a column it reads. Not run here (no database, no CI): pgTAP, `supabase db lint`, `pnpm db:types`, the build, the e2e and the screens at 390, 820 and 1440 px. After review: the after-close line names the month whose file takes the differences (`kinds.pay.after_close.note` `{next}`, `noteUndated` without a month) in the inbox and on the phone; a closed month whose next month is closed too says to download that file again (`diff.nextClosed`, `MonthClosing.nextClosed`); a month with no hours but the month before's differences keeps its file as the main action and says it holds them; the wage sheet's hint names the closed month and the real first day (`wage.sheet.fromHintClosed`, replacing `fromClosed`); the `/design/shell` file follows the screen (`demoPayPathsFor`, `?close=before` and the new `?close=empty` twin with `demoPayDifferences`), checked in `design.spec.ts` by the workbook's sheets; `payroll-close.spec.ts` downloads the next month's file and finds Roni's corrected day with its month and reason on "הפרשים", reads the inbox line's month, and the next month's screen. Next: an accountant's and a lawyer's look at the engine (plan §14) before a real business turns the estimate on.
- Lists L2 fixes from a look at the live demo, on chat/lists-live-fixes over `9f9c8c1` (#91): a range or reading below zero is written with a real minus sign, and the dash is spaced once one is in it ("−25 – −18 °C", which read as "-25–-18"); a main `DockButton` never fades, since the list under the dock showed through a disabled "סיום הרשימה": it turns to glass while it can't be pressed and keeps its colour while it works. - Lists L3, templates and the full send, on chat/lists-templates over `3cdf57d` (P3b, #104), following the lists plan's §1.3, §5, §8 to §11 and §13 with its critique applied, Orel's defaults on #1, and L1, L2 and its fixes (#90 to #92) as built. No migration: L1's `save_list_template`, `archive/restore_list_template` and `send_list` (with L2's `cancel_unstarted_list` for the undo) do it all, so `database.types.ts` is unchanged. `/lists/templates` (list managers; a view session as a manager only reads): the business's templates by the department each goes to, then the rest (to a role, a shift, or chosen each time), an order flagged as one; one group under the other on a phone, two columns on a tablet, one table with who asks for each order on a computer; the main action "יצירת רשימה"; removed ones folded at the end, each with "החזרה", and the one just removed in its editor with its undo on top (`?removed=`); an empty business says what to make. `/lists/templates/[id]` and `/new`: the editor saves itself on every change (a tap at once, typing half a second after it stops or on leaving the field), one save at a time and the newest last, each with the version it last had, so a save someone else's overtook is refused (40001) and the editor opens again with the latest and says so (`?changed=`, keyed by the version); a new template saves once it has a name and an item and takes its own address without drawing the screen again (`history.replaceState`); name and note; items of check, number (unit, range, a "מינוס" toggle for a bound below zero) and note, required or not, up and down on each row, a sheet to add, change or remove one, and the undo of a removal (an item removed and saved comes back as a new row, `itemsForSave`); who it goes to (each time, a department, a shift of the scheduling settings or a role; an order only to another department); "הסרת הרשימה" with its undo on the screen of templates; the main action "שליחת הרשימה עכשיו" saves what's pending, then opens the send screen with it. A recipient that is gone (a removed department, a shift no longer in the settings) opens unchosen and says so, since the database takes it in no save. What the editor doesn't edit yet goes back as it was read: the times (L4) and an order's department, quantities and usual counts (L5) (`templateInputOf`). The console: `/admin/businesses/[slug]/lists`, `/new` and `/[id]` draw the same screens with the team's own actions (`saveTeamListTemplateAction`, `archive/restoreTeamListTemplateAction`, a template only of that business), a "רשימות" row on its settings, and nothing to send. `/lists/send` in full: any template, or "רשימה חדשה" typed now with the same items editor; a person, a department, a published shift from today to a week ahead that hasn't ended ("ערב · שלישי 17.11 · 16:00–23:30 · 4 אנשים", one with nobody on it shown and not chosen; a template that goes to a shift starts at its next one with people), or a role; by when, "בלי שעה", today or tomorrow at a time an hour ahead to start with, refused in words once it has gone by; a note; the button names where it goes ("שליחה למשמרת ערב"); the preview from a tablet up, and on a computer what the person sent today. `/lists` gains "הרשימות של העסק" beside the business's day for list managers, and the main action for anyone who may send, templates or not. Writes bound to the business the screen was drawn for (P3b: `saveListTemplateAction`, `archive/restoreListTemplateAction`, `sendListAction` take it first and refuse another with `businessChanged`, in words). In code: `@domain/lists` gains `numberRange` and `signed` (moved from the list screen), `DraftItem`, `draftItemsOf`, `itemsForSave`, `typedItems`, `templateInputOf`, `templateDraftProblem`, `moved`, `recipientGone`, `templatesByDepartment`, `dueAtOf`, `suggestedDue`, `upcomingShifts`, `SavedTemplate`, and after review `boundOf`, `typedBound` and `readBound` (moved from the item's sheet), `templateStuck`, `sendDefaults` (moved from the send screen) and `dueTimeFor`, with tests; `@db/lists` `saveListTemplate` reads the saved items' ids after the save (the function returns only the id and the version), and once the function has saved, a failure after it carries the template's id (`saved` on `ListWrite`'s failure), `getListTemplate(business, id)`, `getListTemplatesForTeam`, `getListTemplateForTeam`, `getSentLists`; `@db/schedule` `getPublishedShifts`. App-level, not `@ui` (so nothing new for `/design` or CLAUDE.md's list): `ItemsEditor`, `TemplateEditor`, `TemplatesView`, `ListPreview` and `SentToday`. Messages `app.lists.templates.*`, `app.lists.items.*`, `app.lists.templatesRow`, the rest of `app.lists.send` and `admin.settings.lists`, he and en. `/design/shell/lists/templates` (with `?removed=`, `?state=empty` and `?as=viewing`, a view session), `/templates/[id]` (with `?changed=1`, `?as=viewing`, `?state=barGone` for an order whose department was removed, and the removed `t-stock-count`, only read), `/templates/new`, `/send?template=new` and `?state=kitchenGone`, and the console's `/design/console/businesses/[slug]/lists` and `/[id]`, from mock templates in `app/design/shell/lists/data.ts` (already listed in `tooling/hebrew-literals.ts`), all in `design.spec.ts` with a test of the editor's sheets (axe on the new item's and on one already there), the stuck order, the console's twin, a template whose department was removed on the send screen and a typed list sent to a shift. e2e: `list-templates.spec.ts` new (finding and reading the templates and the new item's sheet at every width; on the desktop only, as each writes the shared demo: a template of every kind made, a bound below zero by the toggle and one typed with its minus, saved by itself, reordered by pointer and by keyboard (the focus kept, the move said), an item removed and put back by its undo, renamed, overtaken by a save elsewhere and opened again with the latest (said once, `?changed=` gone from the address), its recipient saved as a department, a role and each time, removed and brought back by the undo and again from the folded list (axe on it), then sent to Noa from its editor; a list typed now sent to next Sunday's evening shift, due tomorrow; the team making one in the console; each removing what it made); `lists.spec.ts`'s view session also reads `/lists/templates` and an editor that changes nothing. After review: a minus typed in a bound is kept and turns the toggle on; a new template whose save may have gone through without its answer stops saving and says so with the way to the business's lists (`templates.failed.createUnknown`), and one whose id came back opens at its own address, so none is made twice; up and down keep the focus on the item's arrows and say where it went (`items.movedTo`, a polite live region); "changed on another screen" goes once a save passes and leaves the address; a template whose department was removed starts the send screen with nobody chosen and says so (`send.gone`), and an order whose department was removed can't be sent by its department (`send.orderGone`, the main action off); choosing today late in the evening starts at a time still to come (`dueTimeFor`, until the person sets one). Deviations from the plan: the editor's desktop is its tablet's two columns (the last sending and the readings come with L4); removing is from the editor, with the undo and a folded "removed" section on the screen of templates; the item ids come from a read after the save rather than from a changed function, which keeps L3 without a migration, and a create lost after the function saved is stopped and said rather than made safe to retry (an id made by the screen would need a migration); a template the database would refuse whatever the editor does (`templateStuck`: an order whose asking department was removed, until L5 lets an order's department be changed, or a time that follows a shift, or goes to a department or a shift, that is gone, until L4 lets the times be edited) opens only to read, says why, and can still be removed or sent; a time sent to someone who has left still reads as "invalid", since the editor doesn't read the people. Checked: Biome, `next typegen`, tsc and vitest over packages, modules, app and tooling (the Windows-only Biome probe aside). Not run here: the build, the e2e (`list-templates.spec.ts` new, `design.spec.ts`, `lists.spec.ts` and `e2e/db.ts` changed), pgTAP (nothing new), and the screens at 390, 820 and 1440 px. Next: L4, the times and the manager's overview.

a business closed on Shabbat gets no list after candle lighting on Friday, and Saturday's at havdalah). `20261110001300_list_schedules.sql` (after P9's `001000`; the plan's `20261112000100` was a placeholder): `private.shabbat_times` (candle lighting and havdalah for the eight cities, every Friday from 6 November 2026 to the end of 2031, 2,152 rows printed by `tooling/shabbat-table.test.ts` with `SHABBAT_TABLE=print`, which checks every row against `@domain/shabbat-times` and fails a year before they run out), `private.closes_on_shabbat` and `private.shabbat_span` (Tel Aviv for no city or one no longer listed), `private.list_occurrences` (the business's own clock, a skipped spring time an hour later with its length kept when that would pass its due time, the later of an autumn hour, minutes around a published shift of its pattern and past midnight, one a day, nothing before the time was saved or on a day before it starts, nothing for a pattern the settings lost, Shabbat's three rules), `private.lists_tick(at_time, for_business)` over `private.lists_tick_business` (each business in its own block; opens once by the schedule's day with the 12-hour catch-up, to the time's recipient or the role manager when nobody is there: a person who left, an empty or unpublished shift, a removed department; the 500 open lists cap under the business's lock; missed, then due, then late, each `for update skip locked`; nothing but missed during a closed business's Shabbat), the job `lists` every minute (off in `seed.sql` with the others), `public.next_list_openings` (three a time from the same function, list managers and the team), `set_list_item` (L1's) logging `lists.out_of_range` when a reading moves outside its range, `private.notify_for_lists` (L1's) with `lists.due` (pushed until due), `lists.late`, `lists.late_team` (the managers who don't hold it, while `tellManagersWhenLate`) and `lists.out_of_range`, and `private.seed_demo_lists` (L2's) ageing the demo's times two days and ticking where the definition says `"scheduledLists": true` (the hosted two, patched here and in `hosted-demo.sql`; `tooling/demo-profiles.test.ts` keeps the local seed's without it), so no reset in a test, and no browser test, depends on the hour; its examples are of templates nothing opened today. Both hosted demo businesses reset. pgTAP `lists_schedule.test.sql` (74). `database.types.ts` gains `next_list_openings`, by hand in the generator's format. In TS: `@domain/list-openings` (the same rules, pure, for a time the editor hasn't saved: `listOpenings`, `nextOpenings`, `dueOpenings`, `wallInstant`, `shabbatTouches`, with tests across a week, both clock changes, shifts past midnight, Friday after candle lighting and Saturday at havdalah), `shabbatSpansFrom` in `@domain/shabbat-times`, and in `@domain/lists` `DraftSchedule`, `draftSchedulesOf`, `schedulesForSave`, `scheduleGone`, `scheduleProblem`, `dayRuns`, `ListSchedule.createdAt`, `SavedTemplate.schedules` and `.openings`, `templateInputOf` taking the editor's times, and `templateStuck` now only for an order (a time pointing at something gone is changed or removed in the editor, which holds its saves until then); the four kinds with their words, inbox and push cases (`late_team` and `out_of_range` ask managers to act); activity cases for `lists.late`, `lists.missed` and `lists.out_of_range`; `@db/lists` `nextListOpenings`, `getListReadings`, `getBusinessPlaceForTeam`, and `saveListTemplate` reading back the times' ids and openings. `DayToggles` in `@ui` (on /design, and the shifts editor uses it). The editor's "מתי היא נשלחת": a row a time (its days in runs, its hour or its place around a shift, by when, to whom, and when it is sent next, from the database once saved and from the rules while not), a sheet to add, change, pause or remove one with its undo, the Shabbat line where it applies; the console's editor too, with the business's place and people read with the secret key. `/lists/today` (list managers; a view session as a manager only reads): late first, open, closed and missed, the day's readings out of range first and the week's, a week strip for the last seven days (`?day=`), cards on a phone, two columns on a tablet, the day's table and the week's readings on a computer; "היום בעסק" on Lists and Today leads there. `/design/shell/lists/today` (with `?day=2026-11-01`, `?state=empty`, `?state=viewing`), the demo templates' times and last night's late closing with a fridge out of range, in `design.spec.ts` with a test of the times sheet and the history. e2e `lists-schedule.spec.ts`: the day and the sheet clean at every width; on a computer, a template of its own, a time saved through the sheet saying when it is sent next, `tickLists` opening it late once, Noa seeing and hearing it and reading the fridge out of range, the manager's day with it first and the week's reading, and the day before. Deviations from the plan: a TS engine beside the SQL one (the task asked for it; the sheet shows a time not yet saved, rows show the database's); "כמה זמן יש" and minutes before a shift are menus rather than a Stepper; scheduled lists that fall back to the managers aren't marked "אין אף אחד במשמרת" on the overview; the editor's desktop has no "last sent" or readings column (the overview has the readings); demo schedules aren't paused in e2e but local resets open none (an opt-in flag on the hosted definitions); CLAUDE.md's "Built so far" still needs `DayToggles` added (left to Orel). Screens: `/lists/[id]` shows on an order what was asked for, a tick gives it, and a stepper under it says how many were given and how many are short, saved half a second after the last tap or on leaving the field (and on leaving the screen); "העברה למחלקה אחרת", a quiet button for whoever holds it or manages lists, opens a sheet of the other live departments with their people (one with nobody is said and not taken), a note, and "העברה ל{name}"; afterwards a word that it went, and the screen stays for whoever still reads it and goes back to Lists otherwise; the timeline shows each pass from where to where with its note, and someone it passed from reads "הרשימה עברה ל…". `/lists` shows the open orders the person's departments asked for that wait elsewhere ("הזמנה אחת מחכה במחלקה אחרת"), in every shape, and someone in more than one department gets a `ChoiceMenu` "כל המחלקות / קופה / רצפת מכירה" (`?department=`, `DepartmentFilter`, the lists the department holds or asked for). `/lists/send` asks "כמה מבקשים" on a template that asks for counts, a stepper an item from its usual count. The template editor edits the order: "מחלקה שמבקשת" (or none) and "מבקשים כמות", a usual count in each check's sheet and on its row; one whose asking department was removed says so on top and holds its saves until another (or none) is chosen, so it never becomes a list of its own by itself, and is no longer only read (`stuck` words gone).
